8: # 1. Training Workflows

11: # Train an IPPO policy (defaults: NYC Manhattan, 120 iters, seed 0, validation every 4 iters)
12: train-ippo env="nyc" iters="120" seed="0" workers="1" eval_interval="4" name="":
13:     #!/usr/bin/env bash
14:     CMD="uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.train --env {{env}} --algo ippo --iterations {{iters}} --seed {{seed}} --num-workers {{workers}}"
15:     if [ "{{eval_interval}}" != "0" ]; then CMD="$CMD --eval-interval {{eval_interval}} --eval-episodes 3"; fi
16:     if [ -n "{{name}}" ]; then CMD="$CMD --name {{name}}"; fi
17:     echo "Executing: $CMD"
18:     $CMD
19:

22:     #!/usr/bin/env bash

1: """Demand distribution shift evaluation suite.
2: 
3: Freezes trained policies and evaluates degradation across shift scenarios
4: defined in docs/demand-shift.md.
5: 
6: Usage:
7:     # Evaluate specific checkpoint:
8:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.shift_eval \\
9:         --checkpoint runs/mappo-nyc-0916-192802/checkpoint.pt \\
10:         --include-baselines \\
11:         --episodes 5
12: 
13:     # Evaluate all available checkpoints in runs/ and baselines:
14:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.shift_eval \\
15:         --all-runs \\
16:         --include-baselines
17: 
18: Outputs:
19:     results/shift_eval/summary.json          — aggregated results by policy × shift
20:     results/shift_eval/{policy}.csv          — per-episode KPI traces
21:     results/shift_eval/degradation_table.md  — markdown summary table
22: """
23: 
24: from __future__ import annotations
25: 
26: import argparse
27: import json
28: import sys
29: from pathlib import Path
30: from typing import Any
31: 
32: import numpy as np
33: 
34: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
35: 
36: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.baselines import GreedyZoneDispatcher, ZoneLPRebalancer
37: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.config import NYCConfig
38: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.env import RideGymAEC
39: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.evaluate import load_trained_policy
40: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.networks.encoder import obs_to_vector
41: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.shifts import SHIFTS, make_shifted_env
42: 
43: RESULTS_DIR = Path("results/shift_eval")
44: EVAL_EPISODES = 5
45: BASE_SEED = 1000
46: 
47: 
48: def evaluate_policy_on_env(
49:     policy: Any,
50:     env: RideGymAEC,
51:     policy_type: str = "checkpoint",
52:     num_episodes: int = 1,
53:     base_seed: int = BASE_SEED,
54:     deterministic: bool = True,
55: ) -> list[dict[str, float]]:
56:     """Run evaluation episodes over fresh seeds. Returns list of KPI dicts."""
57:     episode_results: list[dict[str, float]] = []
58: 
59:     for ep in range(num_episodes):
60:         seed = base_seed + ep
61:         env.reset(seed=seed)
62:         if hasattr(policy, "reset"):
63:             policy.reset(seed=seed)
64: 
65:         acts_idle = acts_dispatch = acts_relocate = 0
66:         total_reward = 0.0
67: 
68:         while env.agents and not env._terminated:
69:             order = [int(z) for z in env._agent_order]
70:             if env.terminations.get(env.agent_selection, False):
71:                 total_reward += float(sum(
72:                     env.rewards.get(f"zone_{slot}", 0.0) for slot in order
73:                 ))
74:                 for _ in order:
75:                     if not env.agents:
76:                         break
77:                     env.step(None)
78:                 continue
79: 
80:             total_reward += float(sum(
81:                 env.rewards.get(f"zone_{slot}", 0.0) for slot in order
82:             ))
83: 
84:             if policy_type == "checkpoint":
85:                 obs_list = []
86:                 mask_list = []
87:                 for slot in order:
88:                     agent = f"zone_{slot}"
89:                     obs = env.observe(agent)
90:                     obs_list.append(obs_to_vector(obs))
91:                     mask_list.append(env.action_mask(agent))
92: 
93:                 actions, _, _ = policy.act(
94:                     np.stack(obs_list), np.stack(mask_list), deterministic=deterministic,
95:                 )
96:                 for slot, act_v in zip(order, actions):
97:                     act_val = int(act_v)
98:                     if act_val == 0:
99:                         acts_idle += 1
100:                     elif 1 <= act_val <= env._max_pending:
101:                         acts_dispatch += 1
102:                     else:
103:                         acts_relocate += 1
104:                     env.step(act_val)
105: 
106:             elif policy_type in ("greedy", "lp"):
107:                 for slot in order:
108:                     agent = f"zone_{slot}"
109:                     obs = env.observe(agent)
110:                     act_val = policy.select_action(agent, env, obs)
111:                     if act_val == 0:
112:                         acts_idle += 1
113:                     elif 1 <= act_val <= env._max_pending:
114:                         acts_dispatch += 1
115:                     else:
116:                         acts_relocate += 1
117:                     env.step(act_val)
118: 
119:             elif policy_type == "random":
120:                 for slot in order:
121:                     agent = f"zone_{slot}"
122:                     mask = env.action_mask(agent)
123:                     valid_acts = np.where(mask)[0]
124:                     act_val = int(np.random.choice(valid_acts)) if len(valid_acts) > 0 else 0
125:                     if act_val == 0:
126:                         acts_idle += 1
127:                     elif 1 <= act_val <= env._max_pending:
128:                         acts_dispatch += 1
129:                     else:
130:                         acts_relocate += 1
131:                     env.step(act_val)
132:             else:
133:                 raise ValueError(f"Unknown policy_type: {policy_type}")
134: 
135:         kpi = env.kpi_summary()
136:         sr = float(kpi["served"]) / max(float(kpi["served"]) + float(kpi["cancelled"]), 1.0)
137:         total_acts = max(acts_idle + acts_dispatch + acts_relocate, 1)
138: 
139:         episode_results.append({
140:             "episode": float(ep + 1),
141:             "seed": float(seed),
142:             "served": float(kpi["served"]),
143:             "cancelled": float(kpi["cancelled"]),
144:             "service_rate": sr,
145:             "avg_wait_min": float(kpi["avg_wait"]),
146:             "empty_km": float(kpi["empty_km"]),
147:             "total_reward": total_reward,
148:             "pct_dispatch": float(100.0 * acts_dispatch / total_acts),
149:             "pct_relocate": float(100.0 * acts_relocate / total_acts),
150:             "pct_idle": float(100.0 * acts_idle / total_acts),
151:         })
152: 
153:     return episode_results
154: 
155: 
156: def evaluate_policy_across_shifts(
157:     policy_name: str,
158:     policy: Any,
159:     policy_type: str,
160:     base_cfg: NYCConfig | None,
161:     shifts: list[tuple],
162:     num_episodes: int = EVAL_EPISODES,
163:     base_seed: int = BASE_SEED,
164:     deterministic: bool = True,
165: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
166:     """Evaluate a single policy across all shift scenarios."""
167:     print(f"\n{'='*70}")
168:     print(f"Evaluating Policy: {policy_name} ({policy_type})")
169:     print(f"{'='*70}")
170: 
171:     results: dict[str, Any] = {}
172: 
173:     for shift_label, w_limit, t_zone, t_pct, shift_param, desc in shifts:
174:         shift_key = shift_label
175:         print(f"  [{shift_label:<4}] {desc:<45} (limit={w_limit})...", end="", flush=True)
176: 
177:         all_episode_results: list[dict[str, float]] = []
178: 
179:         for seed_idx in range(num_episodes):
180:             env = make_shifted_env(
181:                 shift_label,
182:                 w_limit,
183:                 t_zone,
184:                 t_pct,
185:                 shift_param=shift_param,
186:                 seed=base_seed + seed_idx,
187:                 base_cfg=base_cfg,
188:             )
189:             ep_results = evaluate_policy_on_env(
190:                 policy=policy,
191:                 env=env,
192:                 policy_type=policy_type,
193:                 num_episodes=1,
194:                 base_seed=base_seed + seed_idx,
195:                 deterministic=deterministic,
196:             )
197:             all_episode_results.extend(ep_results)
198:             del env
199: 
200:         # Aggregate episode results
201:         if all_episode_results:
202:             avg: dict[str, Any] = {}
203:             for k in all_episode_results[0]:
204:                 vals = [r[k] for r in all_episode_results]
205:                 avg[k] = {
206:                     "mean": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),
207:                     "std": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),
208:                     "min": round(float(np.min(vals)), 6),
209:                     "max": round(float(np.max(vals)), 6),
210:                 }
211:             avg["episodes"] = len(all_episode_results)
212:             results[shift_key] = avg
213: 
214:             sr = avg.get("service_rate", {}).get("mean", 0)
215:             sr_std = avg.get("service_rate", {}).get("std", 0)
216:             empty = avg.get("empty_km", {}).get("mean", 0)
217:             wait = avg.get("avg_wait_min", {}).get("mean", 0)
218:             print(f" -> SR={sr*100:5.1f}% ±{sr_std*100:4.1f}% | Wait={wait:4.2f}m | Empty={empty:5.0f}km")
219: 
220:     return results
221: 
222: 
223: def format_markdown_table(all_results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
224:     """Generate a clean markdown summary table comparing policies across shifts."""
225:     lines = []
226:     lines.append("### Demand Shift Degradation Summary (Service Rate % ± Std)")
227:     lines.append("")
228:     header = "| Policy | " + " | ".join([s[0] for s in SHIFTS]) + " | Mean Retention |"
229:     sep = "|:---| " + " | ".join([":---:" for _ in SHIFTS]) + " | :---: |"
230:     lines.append(header)
231:     lines.append(sep)
232: 
233:     for policy_name, policy_res in all_results.items():
234:         s0_sr = policy_res.get("S0", {}).get("service_rate", {}).get("mean", None)
235:         retention_list = []
236:         row_items = [f"**{policy_name}**"]
237: 
238:         for s_label, _, _, _, _, _ in SHIFTS:
239:             s_data = policy_res.get(s_label, {}).get("service_rate", {})
240:             sr_mean = s_data.get("mean", None)
241:             sr_std = s_data.get("std", 0.0)
242:             if sr_mean is not None:
243:                 row_items.append(f"{sr_mean*100:.1f}% ± {sr_std*100:.1f}%")
244:                 if s0_sr and s0_sr > 0 and s_label != "S0":
245:                     retention_list.append(sr_mean / s0_sr)
246:             else:
247:                 row_items.append("N/A")
248: 
249:         mean_ret = f"{np.mean(retention_list)*100:.1f}%" if retention_list else "N/A"
250:         row_items.append(mean_ret)
251:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row_items) + " |")
252: 
253:     lines.append("")
254:     lines.append("### Empty Repositioning Distance (km / episode)")
255:     lines.append("")
256:     header = "| Policy | " + " | ".join([s[0] for s in SHIFTS]) + " |"
257:     sep = "|:---| " + " | ".join([":---:" for _ in SHIFTS]) + " |"
258:     lines.append(header)
259:     lines.append(sep)
260: 
261:     for policy_name, policy_res in all_results.items():
262:         row_items = [f"**{policy_name}**"]
263:         for s_label, _, _, _, _, _ in SHIFTS:
264:             e_mean = policy_res.get(s_label, {}).get("empty_km", {}).get("mean", None)
265:             if e_mean is not None:
266:                 row_items.append(f"{e_mean:.0f} km")
267:             else:
268:                 row_items.append("N/A")
269:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row_items) + " |")
270: 
271:     lines.append("")
272:     lines.append("### Average Passenger Waiting Time (min)")
273:     lines.append("")
274:     header = "| Policy | " + " | ".join([s[0] for s in SHIFTS]) + " |"
275:     sep = "|:---| " + " | ".join([":---:" for _ in SHIFTS]) + " |"
276:     lines.append(header)
277:     lines.append(sep)
278: 
279:     for policy_name, policy_res in all_results.items():
280:         row_items = [f"**{policy_name}**"]
281:         for s_label, _, _, _, _, _ in SHIFTS:
282:             w_mean = policy_res.get(s_label, {}).get("avg_wait_min", {}).get("mean", None)
283:             if w_mean is not None:
284:                 row_items.append(f"{w_mean:.2f} min")
285:             else:
286:                 row_items.append("N/A")
287:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row_items) + " |")
288: 
289:     return "\n".join(lines)
290:

292: def main() -> None:
293:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Evaluate policies across demand shift suite.")
294:     parser.add_argument(
295:         "--checkpoint",
296:         type=str,
297:         default=None,
298:         help="Path to specific model checkpoint (e.g. runs/mappo-nyc-.../checkpoint.pt)",
299:     )
300:     parser.add_argument(
301:         "--all-runs",
302:         action="store_true",
303:         help="Discover and evaluate all checkpoints found in runs/",
304:     )
305:     parser.add_argument(
306:         "--include-baselines",
307:         action="store_true",
308:         help="Also evaluate non-RL baselines (Greedy & LP)",
309:     )
310:     parser.add_argument(
311:         "--baselines-only",
312:         action="store_true",
313:         help="Only evaluate non-RL baselines (Greedy & LP)",
314:     )
315:     parser.add_argument(
316:         "--episodes",
317:         type=int,
318:         default=EVAL_EPISODES,
319:         help="Number of evaluation seeds per scenario (default: 5)",
320:     )
321:     parser.add_argument(
322:         "--base-seed",
323:         type=int,
324:         default=BASE_SEED,
325:         help="Base seed for evaluation (default: 1000)",
326:     )
327:     parser.add_argument(
328:         "--stochastic",
329:         action="store_true",
330:         help="Use stochastic sampling instead of deterministic argmax",
331:     )
332:     parser.add_argument(
333:         "--out-dir",
334:         type=str,
335:         default=str(RESULTS_DIR),
336:         help="Output directory for results",
337:     )
338:     args = parser.parse_args()
339: 
340:     out_path = Path(args.out_dir)
341:     out_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
342: 
343:     policies_to_evaluate = []
344: 
345:     # 1. Checkpoint discovery
346:     if args.checkpoint:
347:         ckpt_p = Path(args.checkpoint)
348:         if not ckpt_p.exists():
349:             raise FileNotFoundError(f"Checkpoint not found: {ckpt_p}")
350:         p_name = ckpt_p.parent.name
351:         policies_to_evaluate.append((p_name, "checkpoint", ckpt_p))
352:     elif args.all_runs:
353:         runs_dir = Path("runs")
354:         if runs_dir.exists():
355:             for p in sorted(runs_dir.iterdir()):
356:                 ckpt = p / "checkpoint.pt"
357:                 if ckpt.exists():
358:                     policies_to_evaluate.append((p.name, "checkpoint", ckpt))
359:     elif not args.baselines_only:
360:         # Default: pick latest run in runs/
361:         runs_dir = Path("runs")
362:         found = False
363:         if runs_dir.exists():
364:             runs = sorted(runs_dir.iterdir(), key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
365:             for r in runs:
366:                 ckpt = r / "checkpoint.pt"
367:                 if ckpt.exists():
368:                     policies_to_evaluate.append((r.name, "checkpoint", ckpt))
369:                     found = True
370:                     break
371:         if not found:
372:             print("No checkpoints found. Falling back to baselines.")
373:             args.include_baselines = True
374: 
375:     # 2. Baselines
376:     if args.include_baselines or args.baselines_only:
377:         policies_to_evaluate.append(("Greedy-Baseline", "greedy", None))
378:         policies_to_evaluate.append(("LP-Rebalancer", "lp", None))
379: 
380:     all_results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
381: 
382:     for policy_name, p_type, ckpt_or_none in policies_to_evaluate:
383:         if p_type == "checkpoint":
384:             learner, _manifest, eval_env = load_trained_policy(Path(ckpt_or_none))
385:             base_cfg = eval_env._cfg
386:             res = evaluate_policy_across_shifts(
387:                 policy_name=policy_name,
388:                 policy=learner,
389:                 policy_type="checkpoint",
390:                 base_cfg=base_cfg,
391:                 shifts=SHIFTS,
392:                 num_episodes=args.episodes,
393:                 base_seed=args.base_seed,
394:                 deterministic=not args.stochastic,
395:             )
396:         elif p_type == "greedy":
397:             policy = GreedyZoneDispatcher()
398:             base_cfg = NYCConfig()
399:             res = evaluate_policy_across_shifts(
400:                 policy_name=policy_name,
401:                 policy=policy,
402:                 policy_type="greedy",
403:                 base_cfg=base_cfg,
404:                 shifts=SHIFTS,
405:                 num_episodes=args.episodes,
406:                 base_seed=args.base_seed,
407:                 deterministic=True,
408:             )
409:         elif p_type == "lp":
410:             policy = ZoneLPRebalancer()
411:             base_cfg = NYCConfig()
412:             res = evaluate_policy_across_shifts(
413:                 policy_name=policy_name,
414:                 policy=policy,
415:                 policy_type="lp",
416:                 base_cfg=base_cfg,
417:                 shifts=SHIFTS,
418:                 num_episodes=args.episodes,
419:                 base_seed=args.base_seed,
420:                 deterministic=True,
421:             )
422:         elif p_type == "random":
423:             policy = None
424:             base_cfg = NYCConfig()
425:             res = evaluate_policy_across_shifts(

437:

447:                         f.write(

449:                             f"{m_vals['min']},{m_vals['max']}\n"
450:                         )
451: 
452:     # Save summary JSON
453:     summary_path = out_path / "summary.json"
454:     with open(summary_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
455:         json.dump(all_results, f, indent=2)
456:     print(f"\n[Saved JSON summary to {summary_path}]")
457: 
458:     # Generate Markdown table

461:     with open(md_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:

1: """Tune the LP rebalancer baseline against the fleet's own non-RL floor.
2: 
3: The LP baseline is a *tuned* baseline (AGENTS.md rule 4): its two structural
4: knobs are the prediction horizon and the value of filling a predicted shortfall.
5: Both must be swept with the same effort spent on the RL agents, and the sweep
6: must stay off the held-out shifts (AGENTS.md rule 3).
7: 
8: Default sweep scenarios are S0 (train) and S3 (val). S1/S4/S5/S6 are evaluation
9: scenarios: looking at them while choosing hyperparameters would be test-set
10: tuning. Override with ``--shifts`` only if the scenario is not held out.
11: 
12: Usage:
13:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.tune_lp_baseline \\
14:         --lookahead 5 15 30 --penalty-scale 0.25 0.5 1.0 --episodes 3
15: 
16: Outputs:
17:     results/lp_tuning/sweep.json   — every config's per-shift KPIs
18:     results/lp_tuning/sweep.csv    — flat table of the same numbers
19: """
20: 
21: from __future__ import annotations
22: 
23: import argparse
24: import itertools
25: import json
26: from pathlib import Path
27: from typing import Any
28: 
29: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.baselines import (
30:     GreedyZoneDispatcher,
31:     LPBaselineConfig,
32:     ZoneLPRebalancer,
33: )
34: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.config import NYCConfig
35: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.shift_eval import (
36:     SHIFTS,
37:     evaluate_policy_across_shifts,
38: )
39: 
40: OUT_DIR = Path("results/lp_tuning")
41: 
42: # Scenarios that may be inspected while tuning (train + val).
43: TUNING_SHIFTS = ("S0", "S3")
44: 
45: 
46: def _kpi(res: dict[str, Any], shift: str, metric: str) -> float:
47:     return float(res.get(shift, {}).get(metric, {}).get("mean", float("nan")))
48: 
49: 
50: def _mean_over(res: dict[str, Any], shifts: list[str], metric: str) -> float:
51:     vals = [_kpi(res, s, metric) for s in shifts]
52:     vals = [v for v in vals if v == v]  # drop NaN
53:     return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else float("nan")
54: 
55: 
56: def main() -> None:
57:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Sweep LP rebalancer hyperparameters.")
58:     parser.add_argument("--lookahead", type=float, nargs="+", default=[5.0, 15.0, 30.0],
59:                         help="Prediction horizons in minutes (default: 5 15 30)")
60:     parser.add_argument("--penalty-scale", type=float, nargs="+", default=[0.25, 0.5, 1.0],
61:                         help="Deficit penalty, as a multiple of mean inter-zone travel time")
62:     parser.add_argument("--min-idle", type=int, nargs="+", default=[2],
63:                         help="min_idle_to_rebalance values to try (default: 2)")
64:     parser.add_argument("--shifts", type=str, nargs="+", default=list(TUNING_SHIFTS),
65:                         help=f"Shift IDs to tune on (default: {' '.join(TUNING_SHIFTS)}). "
66:                              "Do NOT add held-out evaluation scenarios.")
67:     parser.add_argument("--episodes", type=int, default=3,
68:                         help="Seeds per scenario (default: 3; use >=5 for reported numbers)")
69:     parser.add_argument("--base-seed", type=int, default=1000)
70:     parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=str, default=str(OUT_DIR))
71:     args = parser.parse_args()
72: 
73:     known = {s[0] for s in SHIFTS}
74:     unknown = [s for s in args.shifts if s not in known]
75:     if unknown:
76:         raise SystemExit(f"Unknown shift ID(s): {unknown}. Known: {sorted(known)}")
77: 
78:     held_out = sorted(set(known) - set(args.shifts))

81:     out_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

83:     print(f"Tuning shifts : {args.shifts}")

125:             "name": name,

127:             "deficit_penalty_scale": penalty_scale,
128:             "min_idle_to_rebalance": min_idle,
129:             "seed_count": args.episodes,

135:     # ---------------------------------------------------------------- report

138:         + " | ".join(f"SR {s:<3}" for s in args.shifts)
139:     )
140:     print("\n" + "=" * 92)
141:     print("LP SWEEP")
142:     print("=" * 92)
143:     print(f"{'greedy (reference)':<24} | {_mean_over(greedy_res, list(args.shifts), 'pct_relocate'):7.2f} | "
144:           f"{_mean_over(greedy_res, list(args.shifts), 'empty_km'):8.1f} | "
145:           + " | ".join(f"{100 * _kpi(greedy_res, s, 'service_rate'):5.1f}%" for s in args.shifts))
146:     print("-" * 92)
147:     print(header)
148:     print("-" * 92)
149:     for row in rows:
150:         res = row["results"]
151:         sr_cells = " | ".join(
152:             f"{100 * _kpi(res, s, 'service_rate'):6.1f}%" for s in args.shifts
153:         )
154:         print(
155:             f"{row['name']:<24} | "
156:             f"{_mean_over(res, list(args.shifts), 'pct_relocate'):7.2f} | "
157:             f"{_mean_over(res, list(args.shifts), 'empty_km'):8.1f} | {sr_cells}"
158:         )
159: 
160:     # Acceptance check per AGENTS.md rules 3/4: the LP must actually act, and
161:     # must not lose to greedy on the scenarios it was tuned on.
162:     print("\nAcceptance (tuned-on shifts only):")
163:     for row in rows:
164:         res = row["results"]
165:         reloc = _mean_over(res, list(args.shifts), "pct_relocate")
166:         beats = all(
167:             _kpi(res, s, "service_rate") >= _kpi(greedy_res, s, "service_rate") - 1e-9
168:             for s in args.shifts
169:         )
170:         print(f"  {row['name']:<24} relocates={reloc > 0}  "
171:               f"SR >= greedy on {args.shifts}: {beats}")
172: 
173:     with open(out_path / "sweep.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
174:         json.dump({"greedy_reference": greedy_res, "sweeps": rows}, f, indent=2)
175: 
176:     with open(out_path / "sweep.csv", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
177:         f.write("config,lookahead_minutes,deficit_penalty_scale,min_idle_to_rebalance,"
178:                 "shift,service_rate_mean,service_rate_std,pct_relocate_mean,empty_km_mean,"

Read file /home/prash/college/minor/marl-fleet-rebalancing/src/marl_fleet_rebalancing/experiments/plot_shifts.py, lines 1-128
1: """Plot frozen-policy degradation curves from shift_eval summary JSON.
2: 
3: Usage:
4:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.plot_shifts \\
5:         [summary.json] [--outdir results/figures]
6: 
7: Reads results/shift_eval/summary.json (policy -> shift -> metric stats)
8: and writes one PNG per KPI: service_rate, empty_km, avg_wait_min.
9: Shifts render in taxonomy order (S0..S6).
10: """
11: 
12: from __future__ import annotations
13: 
14: import argparse
15: import json
16: import sys
17: from pathlib import Path
18: 
19: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]))
20: 
21: import matplotlib
22: 
23: matplotlib.use("Agg")
24: import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
25: 
26: # Taxonomy order
27: SHIFT_ORDER = ["S0", "S1", "S2a", "S2b", "S2d", "S2e", "S3", "S4", "S5", "S6"]
28: SHIFT_TICKS = [
29:     "S0\nBase (train)",
30:     "S1\nPermute",
31:     "S2a\n×0.5 orders",
32:     "S2b\n×0.75 orders",
33:     "S2d\n×1.5 orders",
34:     "S2e\n×2.0 orders",
35:     "S3\nVal split",
36:     "S4\nCold zone z1",
37:     "S5\nBurst z0",
38:     "S6\nTest split",
39: ]
40: 
41: KPI_SPECS = {
42:     "service_rate": {"ylabel": "Service Rate (%)", "scale": 100.0, "fmt": "{:.0f}%"},
43:     "empty_km": {"ylabel": "Empty Distance (km/ep)", "scale": 1.0, "fmt": "{:.0f}"},
44:     "avg_wait_min": {"ylabel": "Avg Wait Time (min)", "scale": 1.0, "fmt": "{:.2f}"},
45: }
46: 
47: 
48: def load_summary(path: Path) -> dict:
49:     with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
50:         return json.load(f)
51: 
52: 
53: def plot_kpi(summary: dict, kpi: str, ylabel: str, scale: float, outpath: Path) -> None:
54:     fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, 5.5))
55:     xs: list[int] = []
56:     tick_labels: list[str] = []
57: 
58:     policies = list(summary.keys())
59: 
60:     for i, (key, tick) in enumerate(zip(SHIFT_ORDER, SHIFT_TICKS)):
61:         if any(key in summary.get(p, {}) for p in policies):
62:             xs.append(i)
63:             tick_labels.append(tick)
64: 
65:     # Color palette
66:     colors = plt.cm.tab10(range(len(policies)))
67: 
68:     for idx, policy in enumerate(policies):
69:         pdata = summary.get(policy, {})
70:         x, y, err = [], [], []
71:         for i, key in enumerate(SHIFT_ORDER):
72:             if key not in pdata or kpi not in pdata[key]:
73:                 continue
74:             stat = pdata[key][kpi]
75:             x.append(i)
76:             y.append(stat["mean"] * scale)
77:             err.append(stat["std"] * scale)
78:         if not x:
79:             continue
80: 
81:         style = "--s" if "baseline" in policy.lower() or "greedy" in policy.lower() or "lp" in policy.lower() else "-o"
82:         ax.errorbar(
83:             x,
84:             y,
85:             yerr=err,
86:             fmt=style,
87:             capsize=3,
88:             label=policy,
89:             color=colors[idx],
90:             markersize=5,
91:             linewidth=1.8,
92:         )
93: 
94:     ax.set_xticks(xs)
95:     ax.set_xticklabels(tick_labels, rotation=15, ha="right", fontsize=9)
96:     ax.set_xlabel("Demand Shift Scenario", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
97:     ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
98:     ax.set_title(f"Frozen-Policy Degradation: {ylabel} across Demand Shifts", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
99:     ax.legend(fontsize="small", loc="best", framealpha=0.9)
100:     ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, linestyle="--")
101:     fig.tight_layout()
102:     outpath.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
103:     fig.savefig(outpath, dpi=200)
104:     plt.close(fig)
105:     print(f"Saved figure: {outpath}")
106: 
107: 
108: def main() -> None:
109:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
110:     ap.add_argument("summary", nargs="?", default="results/shift_eval/summary.json")
111:     ap.add_argument("--outdir", default="results/figures")
112:     args = ap.parse_args()
113: 
114:     sum_path = Path(args.summary)
115:     if not sum_path.exists():
116:         print(f"Error: {sum_path} not found.")
117:         sys.exit(1)
118: 
119:     summary = load_summary(sum_path)
120:     outdir = Path(args.outdir)
121: 
122:     for kpi, spec in KPI_SPECS.items():
123:         outpath = outdir / f"degradation_{kpi}.png"
124:         plot_kpi(summary, kpi, spec["ylabel"], spec["scale"], outpath)
125: 
126: 
127: if __name__ == "__main__":
128:     main()

766:                     f"[iter {it:3d}/{train_cfg.iterations}] "
767:                     f"served={record['served_per_ep']:4.0f}/ep (sr={sr*100:4.1f}%) | "
768:                     f"wait={kpis['avg_wait']:4.2f}m | "
769:                     f"empty={record['empty_km_per_ep']:5.1f}km | "
770:                     f"reward={reward_per_ep:+6.1f}/ep"
771:                 )
772:             print(
773:                 f"[timing] rollout={t_roll1 - t_roll0:5.1f}s | "
774:                 f"merge={t_merge1 - t_roll1:5.1f}s | "
775:                 f"update={t_upd1 - t_merge1:5.1f}s"
776:             )
777: 
778:             if it % train_cfg.checkpoint_interval == 0 or it == train_cfg.iterations:
779:                 torch.save(learner.state_dict(), run_dir / "checkpoint.pt")
780:                 # Also keep a versioned copy: checkpoint.pt is overwritten on
781:                 # every interval, so a policy that collapses late in training
782:                 # (e.g. both PPO variants under D25's reward, see
783:                 # docs/shift-results.md) would otherwise leave nothing behind
784:                 # but its final, degraded weights.
785:                 torch.save(
786:                     learner.state_dict(),
787:                     run_dir / f"checkpoint_it{it:04d}.pt",
788:                 )
789: 
790:             # ---- validation-based best-checkpoint tracking ---------------
791:             if train_cfg.eval_interval > 0 and (
792:                 it % train_cfg.eval_interval == 0 or it == train_cfg.iterations
793:             ):
794:                 val = evaluate_deterministic(
795:                     val_env,
796:                     learner,
797:                     episodes=train_cfg.eval_episodes,
798:                     base_seed=train_cfg.eval_seed,
799:                 )
800:                 val_sr = val["service_rate"]
801:                 record["val_service_rate"] = val_sr
802:                 record["val_episodes"] = val["episodes"]
803:                 # Rewrite the just-appended metrics line with the val fields
804:                 # included, so metrics.jsonl stays one JSON object per line.
805:                 metrics_lines = metrics_path.read_text().splitlines()
806:                 metrics_lines[-1] = json.dumps(record)
807:                 metrics_path.write_text("\n".join(metrics_lines) + "\n")
808: 
809:                 if val_sr > best_val_sr:
810:                     best_val_sr = val_sr
811:                     best_val_it = it
812:                     torch.save(learner.state_dict(), run_dir / "checkpoint_best.pt")
813:                     with open(run_dir / "best_checkpoint.json", "w") as f:
814:                         json.dump(
815:                             selection_record(
816:                                 iteration=it,
817:                                 val_service_rate=val_sr,
818:                                 eval_cfg={
819:                                     "val_split": train_cfg.eval_split,
820:                                     "eval_seed": train_cfg.eval_seed,
821:                                     "eval_episodes": train_cfg.eval_episodes,
822:                                 },
823:                             ),
824:                             f, indent=2,
825:                         )
826:                     marker = " (new best)"
827:                 else:
828:                     marker = ""
829:                 print(
830:                     f"[val it {it:3d}] SR={val_sr*100:4.1f}% | "
831:                     f"empty={val['empty_km_per_ep']:5.1f}km | "
832:                     f"wait={val['avg_wait_min']:4.2f}m"
833:                     f" | best={best_val_sr*100:4.1f}% @ it{best_val_it}{marker}"
834:                 )
835: 
836:     finally:
837:         if pool is not None:
838:             pool.close()
839:             pool.join()
840: 
841:     if val_env is not None:
842:         val_env.close()
843: 
844:     if train_cfg.eval_interval > 0 and best_val_it > 0:
845:         print(
846:             f"[train] best validation SR {best_val_sr*100:.1f}% at iteration "
847:             f"{best_val_it} -> {run_dir / 'checkpoint_best.pt'}"
848:         )
849:     print(f"[train] done -> {run_dir}")
850:     return run_dir
851:

22:     ("S3", 500, None, None, "temporal", "Temporal phase shift (val split)"),
23:     ("S4", 500, 1, 60.0, "concentrate", "Cold-zone concentration (60% to z1)"),
24:     ("S5", 500, 0, 20.0, "burst", "Event burst (20% in [20,30]m to z0)"),
25:     ("S6", 500, None, None, "real", "Real held-out demand (test split)"),
26: ]
27: 
28: SHIFTS_DICT: dict[str, tuple[str, int, int | None, float | None, str | None, str]] = {

33: def get_shift_spec(shift_label: str) -> tuple[str, int, int | None, float | None, str | None, str]:
34:     """Retrieve shift definition tuple by label (case-insensitive)."""
35:     key = shift_label.strip().upper()
36:     if key not in SHIFTS_DICT:
37:         valid = list(SHIFTS_DICT.keys())
38:         raise ValueError(f"Unknown shift '{shift_label}'. Valid shifts: {valid}")
39:     return SHIFTS_DICT[key]
40: 
41: 
42: def make_shifted_env(
43:     shift_label: str,
44:     nyc_window_limit: int,
45:     target_zone: int | None,
46:     target_zone_pct: float | None,
47:     shift_param: str | None = None,
48:     seed: int = 0,
49:     base_cfg: NYCConfig | None = None,
50: ) -> RideGymAEC:
51:     """Create an environment with shifted demand configuration."""
52:     if base_cfg is not None:
53:         cfg = copy.deepcopy(base_cfg)
54:     else:
55:         cfg = NYCConfig()
56: 
57:     cfg.nyc_window_limit = nyc_window_limit
58:     cfg.nyc_split = "train"
59:     cfg.order_source = "nyc"
60:     cfg.demand_shift_mode = "none"
61:     cfg.target_zone = None
62:     cfg.target_zone_demand_pct = None
63:     cfg.burst_zone = None
64:     cfg.burst_share_pct = None
65:     cfg.perm_strength = None
66:     cfg.perm_offset = None
67: 
68:     # Mechanism dispatch
69:     if shift_param is not None:
70:         if shift_param == "temporal":
71:             if shift_label.upper() == "S3":
72:                 cfg.nyc_split = "val"
73:             elif shift_label.upper() == "S3B":
74:                 cfg.nyc_split = "test"
75:         elif shift_param == "burst":
76:             cfg.demand_shift_mode = "burst"
77:             cfg.burst_zone = target_zone if target_zone is not None else 0
78:             cfg.burst_share_pct = target_zone_pct if target_zone_pct is not None else 20.0
79:             cfg.burst_start_min = 20.0
80:             cfg.burst_end_min = 30.0
81:         elif shift_param == "permute":
82:             cfg.demand_shift_mode = "permute"
83:             cfg.perm_strength = target_zone_pct if target_zone_pct is not None else 1.0
84:             cfg.perm_offset = target_zone if target_zone is not None else None
85:         elif shift_param == "concentrate":
86:             cfg.demand_shift_mode = "none"
87:             cfg.target_zone = target_zone
88:             cfg.target_zone_demand_pct = target_zone_pct
89:         elif shift_param == "real":
90:             cfg.nyc_split = "test"
91:         else:
92:             raise ValueError(f"Unknown shift_param: {shift_param!r}")
93: 
94:     if shift_param is None and target_zone is not None and target_zone_pct is not None:
95:         cfg.target_zone = target_zone
96:         cfg.target_zone_demand_pct = target_zone_pct
97: 
98:     env = RideGymAEC(config=cfg, seed=seed)
99:     env.reset(seed=seed)
100:     return env

104:     shift_label: str,

107: ) -> RideGymAEC:

**4. Shift spec triple representation**
`config.py:50-60` (7 fields) + `shifts/__init__.py:15-26` (`SHIFTS` 6-tuple) + `shifts/__init__.py:103-118` (`build_shifted_env` wrapper, unused by callers who call `make_shifted_env` directly). `target_zone_pct` unit ambiguous: `shifts/__init__.py:24` uses `60.0`/`20.0` (0-100), `train.py:948-952` and `evaluate.py:336-339` do `if pct>1.0: /100`. 325:         scenario = f"{args.shift.upper()}-{base_scenario}" if args.shift else base_scenario
326:     else:

361:         algo = policy_type.upper()

363:         if policy_type == "greedy":

367:         elif policy_type == "random":

371: 
372:     print(f"Config loaded: Algo={algo} | Scenario={scenario} | Zones={env._num_zones} | Drivers={env._num_drivers}")
373: 
374:     record_animation = args.output is not None or args.interactive
375: 
376:     # Interactive inspect mode: run a single episode — the viewer only shows
377:     # the first one anyway, so running 5 wastes resources.
378:     effective_episodes = 1 if args.interactive else args.episodes
379: 
380:     deterministic = not args.stochastic

382:     print(f"\nRunning {effective_episodes} evaluation episode(s) "

384:           + (" (interactive inspect — single episode)" if args.interactive else ""))
385:     results, recorder = evaluate_policy(

393:     )
394: 
395:     # Compute Aggregate Stats
396:     served_vals = [r["served"] for r in results]
397:     cancelled_vals = [r["cancelled"] for r in results]
398:     sr_vals = [r["service_rate"] * 100 for r in results]
399:     wait_vals = [r["avg_wait_min"] for r in results]
400:     empty_vals = [r["empty_km"] for r in results]
401:     reward_vals = [r["total_reward"] for r in results]
402:     disp_vals = [r["pct_dispatch"] for r in results]
403:     reloc_vals = [r["pct_relocate"] for r in results]
404: 
405:     # Driver-level status counts come from the TrajectoryRecorder, which only
406:     # captures episode 0 (when record_first_episode=True). They are only
407:     # available in single-episode/inspect mode — not meaningful as an

411:     reloc_drv_vals = [r.get("pct_relocate_drivers", 0.0) for r in results] if _all_have_drv else []
412: 
413:     if effective_episodes == 1:
414:         r = results[0]

426:             print(f"  Relocating Drivers: {r['pct_relocate_drivers']:.1f}%")
427:             print(f"  Busy Drivers:       {100.0 - r['pct_idle_drivers'] - r['pct_relocate_drivers']:.1f}%")
428:         print("=" * 60)
429:     else:
430:         print("\n" + "=" * 78)
431:         print(f"EVALUATION SUMMARY ({algo} on {scenario} over {args.episodes} seeds)")
432:         print("=" * 78)

434:         print("-" * 78)

442:         print(f"{'Relocate Actions (%)':<25} | {np.mean(reloc_vals):6.1f}% ± {np.std(reloc_vals):<9.1f}% | [{min(reloc_vals):.1f}%, {max(reloc_vals):.1f}%]")

445:             print(f"{'Relocating Drivers (%)':<25} | {np.mean(reloc_drv_vals):6.1f}% ± {np.std(reloc_drv_vals):<9.1f}% | [{min(reloc_drv_vals):.1f}%, {max(reloc_drv_vals):.1f}%]")
446:         print("=" * 78)

1: """Demand distribution shift evaluation suite.
2: 
3: Freezes trained policies and evaluates degradation across shift scenarios
4: defined in docs/demand-shift.md.
5: 
6: Usage:
7:     # Evaluate specific checkpoint:
8:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.shift_eval \\
9:         --checkpoint runs/mappo-nyc-0916-192802/checkpoint.pt \\
10:         --include-baselines \\
11:         --episodes 5
12: 
13:     # Evaluate all available checkpoints in runs/ and baselines:
14:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.shift_eval \\
15:         --all-runs \\
16:         --include-baselines
17: 
18: Outputs:
19:     results/shift_eval/summary.json          — aggregated results by policy × shift
20:     results/shift_eval/{policy}.csv          — per-episode KPI traces
21:     results/shift_eval/degradation_table.md  — markdown summary table
22: """

24: from __future__ import annotations

32: import numpy as np

36: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.baselines import GreedyZoneDispatcher, ZoneLPRebalancer
37: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.config import NYCConfig
38: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.env import RideGymAEC
39: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.evaluate import load_trained_policy
40: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.networks.encoder import obs_to_vector
41: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.shifts import SHIFTS, make_shifted_env
42: 
43: RESULTS_DIR = Path("results/shift_eval")
44: EVAL_EPISODES = 5
45: BASE_SEED = 1000

47: 
48: def evaluate_policy_on_env(
49:     policy: Any,
50:     env: RideGymAEC,
51:     policy_type: str = "checkpoint",
52:     num_episodes: int = 1,
53:     base_seed: int = BASE_SEED,
54:     deterministic: bool = True,
55: ) -> list[dict[str, float]]:
56:     """Run evaluation episodes over fresh seeds. Returns list of KPI dicts."""
57:     episode_results: list[dict[str, float]] = []
58: 
59:     for ep in range(num_episodes):
60:         seed = base_seed + ep
61:         env.reset(seed=seed)
62:         if hasattr(policy, "reset"):
63:             policy.reset(seed=seed)
64: 
65:         acts_idle = acts_dispatch = acts_relocate = 0
66:         total_reward = 0.0
67: 
68:         while env.agents and not env._terminated:
69:             order = [int(z) for z in env._agent_order]
70:             if env.terminations.get(env.agent_selection, False):
71:                 total_reward += float(sum(
72:                     env.rewards.get(f"zone_{slot}", 0.0) for slot in order
73:                 ))
74:                 for _ in order:
75:                     if not env.agents:
76:                         break
77:                     env.step(None)
78:                 continue
79: 
80:             total_reward += float(sum(
81:                 env.rewards.get(f"zone_{slot}", 0.0) for slot in order
82:             ))
83: 
84:             if policy_type == "checkpoint":
85:                 obs_list = []
86:                 mask_list = []
87:                 for slot in order:
88:                     agent = f"zone_{slot}"
89:                     obs = env.observe(agent)
90:                     obs_list.append(obs_to_vector(obs))
91:                     mask_list.append(env.action_mask(agent))
92: 
93:                 actions, _, _ = policy.act(
94:                     np.stack(obs_list), np.stack(mask_list), deterministic=deterministic,
95:                 )
96:                 for slot, act_v in zip(order, actions):
97:                     act_val = int(act_v)
98:                     if act_val == 0:
99:                         acts_idle += 1
100:                     elif 1 <= act_val <= env._max_pending:
101:                         acts_dispatch += 1
102:                     else:
103:                         acts_relocate += 1
104:                     env.step(act_val)
105: 
106:             elif policy_type in ("greedy", "lp"):
107:                 for slot in order:
108:                     agent = f"zone_{slot}"
109:                     obs = env.observe(agent)
110:                     act_val = policy.select_action(agent, env, obs)
111:                     if act_val == 0:
112:                         acts_idle += 1
113:                     elif 1 <= act_val <= env._max_pending:
114:                         acts_dispatch += 1
115:                     else:
116:                         acts_relocate += 1
117:                     env.step(act_val)
118: 
119:             elif policy_type == "random":
120:                 for slot in order:
121:                     agent = f"zone_{slot}"
122:                     mask = env.action_mask(agent)
123:                     valid_acts = np.where(mask)[0]
124:                     act_val = int(np.random.choice(valid_acts)) if len(valid_acts) > 0 else 0
125:                     if act_val == 0:
126:                         acts_idle += 1
127:                     elif 1 <= act_val <= env._max_pending:
128:                         acts_dispatch += 1
129:                     else:
130:                         acts_relocate += 1
131:                     env.step(act_val)
132:             else:
133:                 raise ValueError(f"Unknown policy_type: {policy_type}")
134: 
135:         kpi = env.kpi_summary()
136:         sr = float(kpi["served"]) / max(float(kpi["served"]) + float(kpi["cancelled"]), 1.0)
137:         total_acts = max(acts_idle + acts_dispatch + acts_relocate, 1)
138: 
139:         episode_results.append({
140:             "episode": float(ep + 1),
141:             "seed": float(seed),
142:             "served": float(kpi["served"]),
143:             "cancelled": float(kpi["cancelled"]),
144:             "service_rate": sr,
145:             "avg_wait_min": float(kpi["avg_wait"]),
146:             "empty_km": float(kpi["empty_km"]),
147:             "total_reward": total_reward,
148:             "pct_dispatch": float(100.0 * acts_dispatch / total_acts),
149:             "pct_relocate": float(100.0 * acts_relocate / total_acts),
150:             "pct_idle": float(100.0 * acts_idle / total_acts),
151:         })
152: 
153:     return episode_results
154:

156: def evaluate_policy_across_shifts(
157:     policy_name: str,
158:     policy: Any,
159:     policy_type: str,
160:     base_cfg: NYCConfig | None,
161:     shifts: list[tuple],
162:     num_episodes: int = EVAL_EPISODES,
163:     base_seed: int = BASE_SEED,
164:     deterministic: bool = True,
165: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
166:     """Evaluate a single policy across all shift scenarios."""
167:     print(f"\n{'='*70}")
168:     print(f"Evaluating Policy: {policy_name} ({policy_type})")
169:     print(f"{'='*70}")
170: 
171:     results: dict[str, Any] = {}
172: 
173:     for shift_label, w_limit, t_zone, t_pct, shift_param, desc in shifts:
174:         shift_key = shift_label
175:         print(f"  [{shift_label:<4}] {desc:<45} (limit={w_limit})...", end="", flush=True)
176: 
177:         all_episode_results: list[dict[str, float]] = []
178: 
179:         for seed_idx in range(num_episodes):
180:             env = make_shifted_env(
181:                 shift_label,
182:                 w_limit,
183:                 t_zone,
184:                 t_pct,
185:                 shift_param=shift_param,
186:                 seed=base_seed + seed_idx,
187:                 base_cfg=base_cfg,
188:             )
189:             ep_results = evaluate_policy_on_env(
190:                 policy=policy,
191:                 env=env,
192:                 policy_type=policy_type,
193:                 num_episodes=1,
194:                 base_seed=base_seed + seed_idx,
195:                 deterministic=deterministic,
196:             )
197:             all_episode_results.extend(ep_results)
198:             del env
199: 
200:         # Aggregate episode results
201:         if all_episode_results:
202:             avg: dict[str, Any] = {}
203:             for k in all_episode_results[0]:
204:                 vals = [r[k] for r in all_episode_results]
205:                 avg[k] = {
206:                     "mean": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 6),
207:                     "std": round(float(np.std(vals)), 6),
208:                     "min": round(float(np.min(vals)), 6),
209:                     "max": round(float(np.max(vals)), 6),
210:                 }
211:             avg["episodes"] = len(all_episode_results)
212:             results[shift_key] = avg
213: 
214:             sr = avg.get("service_rate", {}).get("mean", 0)
215:             sr_std = avg.get("service_rate", {}).get("std", 0)
216:             empty = avg.get("empty_km", {}).get("mean", 0)
217:             wait = avg.get("avg_wait_min", {}).get("mean", 0)
218:             print(f" -> SR={sr*100:5.1f}% ±{sr_std*100:4.1f}% | Wait={wait:4.2f}m | Empty={empty:5.0f}km")
219: 
220:     return results
221: 
222: 
223: def format_markdown_table(all_results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
224:     """Generate a clean markdown summary table comparing policies across shifts."""
225:     lines = []
226:     lines.append("### Demand Shift Degradation Summary (Service Rate % ± Std)")
227:     lines.append("")
228:     header = "| Policy | " + " | ".join([s[0] for s in SHIFTS]) + " | Mean Retention |"
229:     sep = "|:---| " + " | ".join([":---:" for _ in SHIFTS]) + " | :---: |"
230:     lines.append(header)
231:     lines.append(sep)
232: 
233:     for policy_name, policy_res in all_results.items():
234:         s0_sr = policy_res.get("S0", {}).get("service_rate", {}).get("mean", None)
235:         retention_list = []
236:         row_items = [f"**{policy_name}**"]
237: 
238:         for s_label, _, _, _, _, _ in SHIFTS:
239:             s_data = policy_res.get(s_label, {}).get("service_rate", {})
240:             sr_mean = s_data.get("mean", None)
241:             sr_std = s_data.get("std", 0.0)
242:             if sr_mean is not None:
243:                 row_items.append(f"{sr_mean*100:.1f}% ± {sr_std*100:.1f}%")
244:                 if s0_sr and s0_sr > 0 and s_label != "S0":
245:                     retention_list.append(sr_mean / s0_sr)
246:             else:
247:                 row_items.append("N/A")
248: 
249:         mean_ret = f"{np.mean(retention_list)*100:.1f}%" if retention_list else "N/A"
250:         row_items.append(mean_ret)
251:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row_items) + " |")
252: 
253:     lines.append("")
254:     lines.append("### Empty Repositioning Distance (km / episode)")
255:     lines.append("")
256:     header = "| Policy | " + " | ".join([s[0] for s in SHIFTS]) + " |"
257:     sep = "|:---| " + " | ".join([":---:" for _ in SHIFTS]) + " |"
258:     lines.append(header)
259:     lines.append(sep)
260: 
261:     for policy_name, policy_res in all_results.items():
262:         row_items = [f"**{policy_name}**"]
263:         for s_label, _, _, _, _, _ in SHIFTS:
264:             e_mean = policy_res.get(s_label, {}).get("empty_km", {}).get("mean", None)
265:             if e_mean is not None:
266:                 row_items.append(f"{e_mean:.0f} km")
267:             else:
268:                 row_items.append("N/A")
269:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row_items) + " |")
270: 
271:     lines.append("")
272:     lines.append("### Average Passenger Waiting Time (min)")
273:     lines.append("")
274:     header = "| Policy | " + " | ".join([s[0] for s in SHIFTS]) + " |"
275:     sep = "|:---| " + " | ".join([":---:" for _ in SHIFTS]) + " |"
276:     lines.append(header)
277:     lines.append(sep)
278: 
279:     for policy_name, policy_res in all_results.items():
280:         row_items = [f"**{policy_name}**"]
281:         for s_label, _, _, _, _, _ in SHIFTS:
282:             w_mean = policy_res.get(s_label, {}).get("avg_wait_min", {}).get("mean", None)
283:             if w_mean is not None:
284:                 row_items.append(f"{w_mean:.2f} min")
285:             else:
286:                 row_items.append("N/A")
287:         lines.append("| " + " | ".join(row_items) + " |")
288: 
289:     return "\n".join(lines)

293:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Evaluate policies across demand shift suite.")
294:     parser.add_argument(

343:     policies_to_evaluate = []

345:     # 1. Checkpoint discovery

124:     alpha: float = 0.2   # weight for passenger waiting time (D25)

33: def get_shift_spec(shift_label: str) -> tuple[str, int, int | None, float | None, str | None, str]:
34:     """Retrieve shift definition tuple by label (case-insensitive)."""
35:     key = shift_label.strip().upper()
36:     if key not in SHIFTS_DICT:
37:         valid = list(SHIFTS_DICT.keys())
38:         raise ValueError(f"Unknown shift '{shift_label}'. Valid shifts: {valid}")
39:     return SHIFTS_DICT[key]
40: 
41: 
42: def make_shifted_env(
43:     shift_label: str,
44:     nyc_window_limit: int,
45:     target_zone: int | None,
46:     target_zone_pct: float | None,
47:     shift_param: str | None = None,
48:     seed: int = 0,
49:     base_cfg: NYCConfig | None = None,
50: ) -> RideGymAEC:
51:     """Create an environment with shifted demand configuration."""
52:     if base_cfg is not None:
53:         cfg = copy.deepcopy(base_cfg)
54:     else:
55:         cfg = NYCConfig()
56: 
57:     cfg.nyc_window_limit = nyc_window_limit
58:     cfg.nyc_split = "train"
59:     cfg.order_source = "nyc"
60:     cfg.demand_shift_mode = "none"
61:     cfg.target_zone = None
62:     cfg.target_zone_demand_pct = None
63:     cfg.burst_zone = None
64:     cfg.burst_share_pct = None
65:     cfg.perm_strength = None
66:     cfg.perm_offset = None
67: 
68:     # Mechanism dispatch
69:     if shift_param is not None:
70:         if shift_param == "temporal":
71:             if shift_label.upper() == "S3":
72:                 cfg.nyc_split = "val"
73:             elif shift_label.upper() == "S3B":
74:                 cfg.nyc_split = "test"
75:         elif shift_param == "burst":
76:             cfg.demand_shift_mode = "burst"
77:             cfg.burst_zone = target_zone if target_zone is not None else 0
78:             cfg.burst_share_pct = target_zone_pct if target_zone_pct is not None else 20.0
79:             cfg.burst_start_min = 20.0
80:             cfg.burst_end_min = 30.0
81:         elif shift_param == "permute":
82:             cfg.demand_shift_mode = "permute"
83:             cfg.perm_strength = target_zone_pct if target_zone_pct is not None else 1.0
84:             cfg.perm_offset = target_zone if target_zone is not None else None
85:         elif shift_param == "concentrate":
86:             cfg.demand_shift_mode = "none"
87:             cfg.target_zone = target_zone
88:             cfg.target_zone_demand_pct = target_zone_pct
89:         elif shift_param == "real":

91:         else:
92:             raise ValueError(f"Unknown shift_param: {shift_param!r}")
93: 
94:     if shift_param is None and target_zone is not None and target_zone_pct is not None:
95:         cfg.target_zone = target_zone
96:         cfg.target_zone_demand_pct = target_zone_pct
97: 
98:     env = RideGymAEC(config=cfg, seed=seed)
99:     env.reset(seed=seed)
100:     return env

7: Every run logs: full config (env + algo), seed(s), demand scenario ID,
8: and per-iteration KPIs (AGENTS.md experiment rules). Checkpoints are written
9: to the run dir but never committed (git-ignored).
10: """

37: @dataclass
38: class TrainConfig:
39:     """Training-run parameters (everything logged, nothing hardcoded)."""
40: 
41:     env_config: EnvConfig
42:     algo_config: IPPOConfig | MAPPOConfig
43:     seed: int = 0

46:     out_root: Path = Path("runs")
47:     run_name: str | None = None
48:     checkpoint_interval: int = 10
49:     # Evaluate deterministically every N iterations and save checkpoint_best.pt
50:     # when validation service rate improves (0 = disabled). Selection must run
51:     # on the frozen, deterministic protocol — training SR is stochastic and
52:     # rewarded a collapsed policy 23 points above its frozen value.
53:     # Selection runs in a DEDICATED env on the held-out NYC val split (never
54:     # the training env: seeded val resets used to rewrite the training RNG
55:     # stream, see tests/test_training_validation.py).
56:     eval_interval: int = 0
57:     eval_episodes: int = 3
58:     eval_seed: int = 9000  # disjoint from the training seed stream
59:     eval_split: str = "val"  # held-out split used for selection (never "train")
60:     eval_window_limit: int | None = 500  # matches the training window budget
61:     verbose: bool = False
62:     num_workers: int = 1  # parallel rollout envs (1 = serial, current behavior)
63:

67:             "algo_config": self.algo_config.to_dict(),
68:             "seed": self.seed,

82:     return int(round(cfg.horizon / cfg.dt))

84: 
85: def evaluate_deterministic(
86:     env: RideGymAEC,
87:     learner: EvalLearner,
88:     episodes: int,
89:     base_seed: int,
90: ) -> dict:
91:     """Frozen-policy deterministic evaluation on fresh seeds (no learning).
92: 
93:     Used for best-checkpoint selection: training KPIs come from stochastic
94:     rollouts on the training stream, which made a mid-training policy look 23
95:     points better frozen than training (see docs/shift-results.md, IPPO
96:     collapse). Selection must use the same protocol as the final evaluation:
97:     deterministic argmax actions on unseen seeds.
98: 
99:     Runs on a DEDICATED validation env (see ``_make_validation_env``), never
100:     on the training env — resetting the training env with the val seeds
101:     rewrote its RNG stream and silently changed subsequent training episodes.
102:     The caller owns both envs; this function never resets anything after the
103:     val episodes.
104:     """
105:     served = cancelled = 0
106:     empty_km = 0.0
107:     wait_sum = 0.0
108:     for i in range(episodes):
109:         env.reset(seed=base_seed + i)
110:         while env.agents and not env._terminated:
111:             order = [int(z) for z in env._agent_order]
112:             if env.terminations.get(env.agent_selection, False):
113:                 for _ in order:
114:                     if not env.agents:
115:                         break
116:                     env.step(None)
117:                 continue
118:             obs_list, mask_list = [], []
119:             for slot in order:
120:                 agent = f"zone_{slot}"
121:                 obs_list.append(obs_to_vector(env.observe(agent)))
122:                 mask_list.append(env.action_mask(agent))
123:             actions, _, _ = learner.act(
124:                 np.stack(obs_list), np.stack(mask_list), deterministic=True,
125:             )
126:             for slot, act_v in zip(order, actions):
127:                 env.step(int(act_v))
128:         kpi = env.kpi_summary()
129:         served += int(kpi["served"])
130:         cancelled += int(kpi["cancelled"])
131:         empty_km += float(kpi["empty_km"])
132:         wait_sum += float(kpi["avg_wait"])
133:     n = max(episodes, 1)
134:     return {
135:         "episodes": episodes,
136:         "service_rate": served / max(served + cancelled, 1),
137:         "served_per_ep": served / n,
138:         "empty_km_per_ep": empty_km / n,
139:         "avg_wait_min": wait_sum / n,
140:     }
141: 
142: 
143: def _make_validation_env(
144:     train_cfg: TrainConfig,
145:     seed: int,
146: ) -> RideGymAEC:
147:     """Build the dedicated selection env: same sim, held-out demand split.
148: 
149:     Deep-copies the training env config and overrides ONLY the demand split
150:     (and window limit if set), so selection sees the held-out ``val`` windows
151:     under an identical fleet/network/reward. A fresh env instance also means
152:     selection can never perturb the training env's RNG stream or window
153:     cursor (regression-tested: tests/test_training_validation.py).
154:     """
155:     val_cfg = copy.deepcopy(train_cfg.env_config)
156:     val_cfg.nyc_split = train_cfg.eval_split
157:     if (
158:         train_cfg.eval_window_limit is not None
159:         and hasattr(val_cfg, "nyc_window_limit")
160:     ):
161:         val_cfg.nyc_window_limit = train_cfg.eval_window_limit
162:     return RideGymAEC(config=val_cfg, seed=seed)
163: 
164: 
165: def selection_record(
166:     iteration: int,
167:     val_service_rate: float,
168:     eval_cfg: dict,
169: ) -> dict:
170:     """Provenance block for a selected checkpoint: iteration + how it was picked.
171: 
172:     Written to ``best_checkpoint.json`` so every best checkpoint is auditable
173:     (which split, which seeds, how many episodes) without reading code.
174:     """
175:     return {
176:         "iteration": iteration,
177:         "val_service_rate": val_service_rate,
178:         "val_split": eval_cfg["val_split"],
179:         "eval_seed": eval_cfg["eval_seed"],
180:         "eval_episodes": eval_cfg["eval_episodes"],

1: """Tune the LP rebalancer baseline against the fleet's own non-RL floor.
2: 
3: The LP baseline is a *tuned* baseline (AGENTS.md rule 4): its two structural
4: knobs are the prediction horizon and the value of filling a predicted shortfall.
5: Both must be swept with the same effort spent on the RL agents, and the sweep
6: must stay off the held-out shifts (AGENTS.md rule 3).
7: 
8: Default sweep scenarios are S0 (train) and S3 (val). S1/S4/S5/S6 are evaluation
9: scenarios: looking at them while choosing hyperparameters would be test-set
10: tuning. Override with ``--shifts`` only if the scenario is not held out.
11: 
12: Usage:
13:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.tune_lp_baseline \\
14:         --lookahead 5 15 30 --penalty-scale 0.25 0.5 1.0 --episodes 3
15: 
16: Outputs:
17:     results/lp_tuning/sweep.json   — every config's per-shift KPIs
18:     results/lp_tuning/sweep.csv    — flat table of the same numbers
19: """
20: 
21: from __future__ import annotations
22: 
23: import argparse
24: import itertools
25: import json
26: from pathlib import Path
27: from typing import Any
28: 
29: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.baselines import (
30:     GreedyZoneDispatcher,
31:     LPBaselineConfig,
32:     ZoneLPRebalancer,
33: )
34: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.config import NYCConfig
35: from marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.shift_eval import (
36:     SHIFTS,
37:     evaluate_policy_across_shifts,
38: )
39: 
40: OUT_DIR = Path("results/lp_tuning")
41: 
42: # Scenarios that may be inspected while tuning (train + val).
43: TUNING_SHIFTS = ("S0", "S3")
44: 
45: 
46: def _kpi(res: dict[str, Any], shift: str, metric: str) -> float:
47:     return float(res.get(shift, {}).get(metric, {}).get("mean", float("nan")))
48: 
49: 
50: def _mean_over(res: dict[str, Any], shifts: list[str], metric: str) -> float:
51:     vals = [_kpi(res, s, metric) for s in shifts]
52:     vals = [v for v in vals if v == v]  # drop NaN
53:     return sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else float("nan")
54: 
55: 
56: def main() -> None:
57:     parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Sweep LP rebalancer hyperparameters.")
58:     parser.add_argument("--lookahead", type=float, nargs="+", default=[5.0, 15.0, 30.0],
59:                         help="Prediction horizons in minutes (default: 5 15 30)")
60:     parser.add_argument("--penalty-scale", type=float, nargs="+", default=[0.25, 0.5, 1.0],
61:                         help="Deficit penalty, as a multiple of mean inter-zone travel time")
62:     parser.add_argument("--min-idle", type=int, nargs="+", default=[2],
63:                         help="min_idle_to_rebalance values to try (default: 2)")
64:     parser.add_argument("--shifts", type=str, nargs="+", default=list(TUNING_SHIFTS),
65:                         help=f"Shift IDs to tune on (default: {' '.join(TUNING_SHIFTS)}). "
66:                              "Do NOT add held-out evaluation scenarios.")
67:     parser.add_argument("--episodes", type=int, default=3,
68:                         help="Seeds per scenario (default: 3; use >=5 for reported numbers)")
69:     parser.add_argument("--base-seed", type=int, default=1000)
70:     parser.add_argument("--out-dir", type=str, default=str(OUT_DIR))
71:     args = parser.parse_args()
72: 
73:     known = {s[0] for s in SHIFTS}
74:     unknown = [s for s in args.shifts if s not in known]
75:     if unknown:
76:         raise SystemExit(f"Unknown shift ID(s): {unknown}. Known: {sorted(known)}")
77: 
78:     held_out = sorted(set(known) - set(args.shifts))

81:     out_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

83:     print(f"Tuning shifts : {args.shifts}")

85:     print(f"Seeds/scenario: {args.episodes} (base_seed={args.base_seed})")
86:     if args.episodes < 5:
87:         print("NOTE: <5 seeds is debugging output, not a reportable result (AGENTS.md rule 1).")
88: 
89:     base_cfg = NYCConfig()
90: 
91:     # Reference floor: the tuned baseline must beat this with real effort.
92:     print("\n" + "=" * 92)

95:     greedy_res = evaluate_policy_across_shifts(

103:     )
104:

106:     for lookahead, penalty_scale, min_idle in itertools.product(

108:     ):

114:         name = f"lp_la{lookahead:g}_p{penalty_scale:g}_mi{min_idle}"
115:         res = evaluate_policy_across_shifts(

120:             shifts=shifts,
121:             num_episodes=args.episodes,
122:             base_seed=args.base_seed,
123:         )
124:         rows.append({
125:             "name": name,
126:             "lookahead_minutes": lookahead,
127:             "deficit_penalty_scale": penalty_scale,
128:             "min_idle_to_rebalance": min_idle,
129:             "seed_count": args.episodes,
130:             "base_seed": args.base_seed,
131:             "shifts": list(args.shifts),
132:             "results": res,
133:         })
134: 
135:     # ---------------------------------------------------------------- report
136:     header = (
137:         f"{'config':<24} | {'reloc%':>7} | {'empty_km':>8} | "
138:         + " | ".join(f"SR {s:<3}" for s in args.shifts)
139:     )
140:     print("\n" + "=" * 92)
141:     print("LP SWEEP")
142:     print("=" * 92)
143:     print(f"{'greedy (reference)':<24} | {_mean_over(greedy_res, list(args.shifts), 'pct_relocate'):7.2f} | "
144:           f"{_mean_over(greedy_res, list(args.shifts), 'empty_km'):8.1f} | "
145:           + " | ".join(f"{100 * _kpi(greedy_res, s, 'service_rate'):5.1f}%" for s in args.shifts))
146:     print("-" * 92)
147:     print(header)

149:     for row in rows:
150:         res = row["results"]
151:         sr_cells = " | ".join(
152:             f"{100 * _kpi(res, s, 'service_rate'):6.1f}%" for s in args.shifts
153:         )
154:         print(
155:             f"{row['name']:<24} | "
156:             f"{_mean_over(res, list(args.shifts), 'pct_relocate'):7.2f} | "
157:             f"{_mean_over(res, list(args.shifts), 'empty_km'):8.1f} | {sr_cells}"
158:         )
159: 
160:     # Acceptance check per AGENTS.md rules 3/4: the LP must actually act, and
161:     # must not lose to greedy on the scenarios it was tuned on.
162:     print("\nAcceptance (tuned-on shifts only):")
163:     for row in rows:
164:         res = row["results"]
165:         reloc = _mean_over(res, list(args.shifts), "pct_relocate")
166:         beats = all(

168:             for s in args.shifts

170:         print(f"  {row['name']:<24} relocates={reloc > 0}  "
171:               f"SR >= greedy on {args.shifts}: {beats}")
172: 
173:     with open(out_path / "sweep.json", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
174:         json.dump({"greedy_reference": greedy_res, "sweeps": rows}, f, indent=2)
175: 
176:     with open(out_path / "sweep.csv", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
177:         f.write("config,lookahead_minutes,deficit_penalty_scale,min_idle_to_rebalance,"

1: """Plot frozen-policy degradation curves from shift_eval summary JSON.
2: 
3: Usage:
4:     uv run python -m marl_fleet_rebalancing.experiments.plot_shifts \\
5:         [summary.json] [--outdir results/figures]
6: 
7: Reads results/shift_eval/summary.json (policy -> shift -> metric stats)
8: and writes one PNG per KPI: service_rate, empty_km, avg_wait_min.
9: Shifts render in taxonomy order (S0..S6).
10: """
11:

13:

15: import json

33:     "S2d\n×1.5 orders",
34:     "S2e\n×2.0 orders",
35:     "S3\nVal split",

44:     "avg_wait_min": {"ylabel": "Avg Wait Time (min)", "scale": 1.0, "fmt": "{:.2f}"},

51: 
52: 
53: def plot_kpi(summary: dict, kpi: str, ylabel: str, scale: float, outpath: Path) -> None:
54:     fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(11, 5.5))
55:     xs: list[int] = []
56:     tick_labels: list[str] = []
57: 
58:     policies = list(summary.keys())
59: 
60:     for i, (key, tick) in enumerate(zip(SHIFT_ORDER, SHIFT_TICKS)):
61:         if any(key in summary.get(p, {}) for p in policies):
62:             xs.append(i)
63:             tick_labels.append(tick)
64: 
65:     # Color palette
66:     colors = plt.cm.tab10(range(len(policies)))
67: 
68:     for idx, policy in enumerate(policies):
69:         pdata = summary.get(policy, {})
70:         x, y, err = [], [], []
71:         for i, key in enumerate(SHIFT_ORDER):
72:             if key not in pdata or kpi not in pdata[key]:
73:                 continue
74:             stat = pdata[key][kpi]
75:             x.append(i)
76:             y.append(stat["mean"] * scale)
77:             err.append(stat["std"] * scale)
78:         if not x:
79:             continue
80: 
81:         style = "--s" if "baseline" in policy.lower() or "greedy" in policy.lower() or "lp" in policy.lower() else "-o"
82:         ax.errorbar(
83:             x,
84:             y,
85:             yerr=err,
86:             fmt=style,
87:             capsize=3,
88:             label=policy,
89:             color=colors[idx],
90:             markersize=5,
91:             linewidth=1.8,
92:         )
93: 
94:     ax.set_xticks(xs)
95:     ax.set_xticklabels(tick_labels, rotation=15, ha="right", fontsize=9)
96:     ax.set_xlabel("Demand Shift Scenario", fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
97:     ax.set_ylabel(ylabel, fontsize=11, fontweight="bold")
98:     ax.set_title(f"Frozen-Policy Degradation: {ylabel} across Demand Shifts", fontsize=12, fontweight="bold")
99:     ax.legend(fontsize="small", loc="best", framealpha=0.9)
100:     ax.grid(True, alpha=0.3, linestyle="--")
101:     fig.tight_layout()
102:     outpath.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
103:     fig.savefig(outpath, dpi=200)
104:     plt.close(fig)
105:     print(f"Saved figure: {outpath}")
106: 
107: 
108: def main() -> None:
109:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
110:     ap.add_argument("summary", nargs="?", default="results/shift_eval/summary.json")
111:     ap.add_argument("--outdir", default="results/figures")
112:     args = ap.parse_args()
113: 
114:     sum_path = Path(args.summary)
115:     if not sum_path.exists():
116:         print(f"Error: {sum_path} not found.")
117:         sys.exit(1)
118: 
119:     summary = load_summary(sum_path)
120:     outdir = Path(args.outdir)
121: 
122:     for kpi, spec in KPI_SPECS.items():
123:         outpath = outdir / f"degradation_{kpi}.png"
124:         plot_kpi(summary, kpi, spec["ylabel"], spec["scale"], outpath)
125: 
126: 
127: if __name__ == "__main__":
128:     main()

## Assistant

_Thinking:_



DevX audit — no code changed, read-only:

## A. CLI flag sprawl

**1. `train.py:853-915` — 28 flags in one command.**
Env/reward overrides duplicated from `config.py`: `--alpha`, `--beta`, `--gamma`, `--team-share`, `--cancel-penalty`, `--prospective-bonus`, `--num-drivers`, `--max-dispatch-radius`, `--relocation-scope`, `--nyc-window-limit`, `--target-zone`, `--target-zone-demand-pct`. This violates `AGENTS.md` ("all params in config"). Every new config field becomes 3 edits: dataclass + train flag + evaluate flag. Should be one `--config yaml` + `--override key=value`.

**2. `evaluate.py:270-301` — 15 flags mixing 3 jobs.**
Metrics (`--episodes`, `--base-seed`, `--stochastic`), demand override (`--shift`, `--target-zone`, `--num-drivers`, `--max-dispatch-radius`), and rendering (`-i/--interactive`, `--heatmap`, `-o/--output/--out-mp4`, `--fps`). One file does eval + viewer + video export. `--output` has 3 aliases for one thing. Split into `eval` vs `render`/`view`.

**3. `shift_eval.py:293-338` — 8 more flags, overlapping.**
`--episodes`, `--base-seed`, `--stochastic`, `--out-dir` duplicate evaluate.py. Plus 4-way checkpoint selection: `--checkpoint` vs `--all-runs` vs `--include-baselines` vs `--baselines-only` — easy to pass contradictory combos (e.g. `--checkpoint X --all-runs` silently ignores one).

**4. `tune_lp_baseline.py:58-70`, `plot_shifts.py:109-111` — 2 more CLIs.**
`--episodes/--base-seed/--out-dir` again. Plot takes positional `summary` + `--outdir` with different naming convention (`outdir` vs `out-dir` vs `out-root`).

## B. Duplicated logic (same behavior, 3+ copies)

**5. Three eval loops.**
- `train.py:85-140` `evaluate_deterministic`
- `evaluate.py:128-265` `evaluate_policy`
- `shift_eval.py:48-153` `evaluate_policy_on_env`
All walk `env._agent_order`, step each zone, drain termination, count `acts_idle/dispatch/relocate`, aggregate `kpi_summary()`. They differ in reward summation and recorder handling for no reason. One shared `rollout()` in a lib would kill ~200 lines.

**6. Five env constructors.**
`train.py:_make_env`, `train.py:_make_validation_env`, `evaluate.py:load_trained_policy` + baseline branch, `shifts/__init__.py:make_shifted_env` + `build_shifted_env`. Same deep-copy + override pattern. `evaluate.py:345-359` even re-implements shift dispatch that `build_shifted_env` already does.

**7. Three reporters.**
`evaluate.py:395-446` prints mean±std table, `shift_eval.py:223-289` builds markdown tables, `tune_lp_baseline.py:136-171` prints sweep table. Same `service_rate/empty_km/wait` formatting, three implementations.

**8. Pct normalization copy-paste ×3.**
`train.py:948-952`, `evaluate.py:74-77`, `evaluate.py:336-339`: identical `if pct > 1.0: pct /= 100`. Should be a helper or removed (pick one unit).

**9. `IPPOConfig` vs `MAPPOConfig` identical.**
`agents/ippo.py:21-45` and `agents/mappo.py:27-51` — same 10 fields, same defaults. Only docstring differs. Same for `IPPO.update` vs `MAPPO.update` — ~80 lines duplicated except 3 lines (actor vs critic forward). Should be one `PPOConfig` + shared update.

## C. Shift-spec drift risk

**10. `shifts/__init__.py:15-30` vs `plot_shifts.py:27-39`.**
`SHIFTS` + `SHIFTS_DICT` + `get_shift_spec` is the source of truth. Plot hardcodes separate `SHIFT_ORDER`/`SHIFT_TICKS` — already diverged (no `S3B` handling, labels differ from `SHIFTS` desc). `evaluate.py:292` help text lists shifts manually (`S0, S1, S2a...`) — will go stale.

**11. Two shift-env helpers doing one thing.**
`make_shifted_env` (7 args) and `build_shifted_env` (3 args, calls the first). Callers use both. Keep one.

## D. justfile duplication

**12. `justfile:12-27` — `train-ippo` vs `train-mappo` identical except `--algo`.**
Should be `train algo="ippo"`. Same for `eval-lp`/`eval-greedy` (`:52-59`), `view-lp`/`view-greedy` (`:76-83`) — only `--policy` differs. `render-lp` exists but `render-greedy` doesn't — asymmetric for no reason.
`eval` + `view` + `render` all build the same `evaluate ... --checkpoint ... --shift ...` string with bash `if` guards — shell re-implements argparse defaults.

## E. Misc tech debt

**13. Backward-compat shims in hot path.**
`evaluate.py:51-53` manifest vs config.json fallback, `:107-119` weight-shape inspection to resize obs/state dim. Needed once, now permanent complexity.

**14. `sys.path.insert` hacks.**
`shift_eval.py:34`, `plot_shifts.py:19` — breaks when run as installed package.

**15. Constants scattered.**
`shift_eval.py:43-45` (`RESULTS_DIR/EVAL_EPISODES/BASE_SEED`), `tune_lp_baseline.py:40` (`OUT_DIR`), `train.py:56-60` (eval defaults). Same seed `1000` vs `9000` magic numbers in 4 files.

**16. Bandra/NYC `if/else` everywhere.**
`train.py:925`, `evaluate.py:328-331`, `shifts:55` — preset name branching instead of config-file path.

Net: ~28+15+8+7 flags across 4 CLIs, 3 eval loops, 5 env builders, 2 justfile templates ×6. The fix order I'd suggest: single YAML config → one `eval_loop()` → one `ShiftSpec` → slim justfile. Say the word and I'll scope it.
