30: | **LP-Rebalancer** (D27, tuned) | **70.5 ± 1.8** | **56.0 ± 1.8** | **82.5 ± 4.4** | **77.1 ± 1.7** | **63.9 ± 2.9** | **53.6 ± 1.7** | **70.6 ± 1.8** | **32.0 ± 1.5** | **69.1 ± 2.0** | **69.3 ± 3.4** | **90.5%** |
31: 
32: **Read the ippo-it75 row with care.** It is the frozen iteration-75 checkpoint
33: (the pre-collapse peak window of `ippo-nyc-s0-95it-ck5`), evaluated on the
34: shift suite. Its *training* service rate at that point was 74.4% — but its
35: frozen evaluation S0 is only 51.5%, below greedy. Two things explain the gap:
36: training metrics are measured on stochastic rollouts in the training window
37: mix, while evaluation is deterministic on fresh seeds; and the checkpoint was
38: selected by peak training SR, not by validation SR, so it may already sit
39: partway into the collapse. The honest statement is: **no IPPO checkpoint has
40: yet been produced whose frozen evaluation beats greedy**, while MAPPO-100it
41: (57.2%) does. Whether that is an IPPO-vs-MAPPO difference or a
42: checkpoint-selection artifact cannot be settled until early stopping selects
43: checkpoints by validation performance for both algos. Until then, H1-H4 have
44: no answer, and the ippo-it75 row is a data point, not a comparison.
45: 
46: Empty km / episode and avg wait are in `results/shift_eval/degradation_table.md`.
47: Figures: `results/figures/degradation_{service_rate,empty_km,avg_wait_min}.png`.
48: 
49: ## What this says
50: 
51: 1. **The tuned baseline leads on every shift.** LP beats the best RL policy by
52:    13.3 points on S0 and by 12.6–23.3 points on every other shift, at the
53:    highest retention (90.5%). No RL policy is currently competitive with a
54:    well-tuned rolling-horizon LP. This is the headline fact, not a footnote.
55: 2. **The baseline's advantage is bought with repositioning.** LP burns 815 km
56:    of empty travel on S0 versus 119 km for greedy; MAPPO-185716 burns 1829 km
57:    for 13 fewer points of service rate, i.e. it relocates harder than the LP
58:    and gets much less for it.
59: 3. **More training made it worse, and turned relocation off.** 100 → 400
60:    iterations drops S0 service rate 57.2% → 52.8%, and `pct_relocate` falls
61:    6.22% → 0.01%. The 400-iteration runs are effectively greedy dispatchers
62:    with a learned dispatch choice. Retention looks slightly *better* (86.9% vs
63:    86.4%) only because the in-distribution starting point fell.
64: 
65:    **Diagnosis (2026-09-16, from metrics.jsonl):** this is not an exploration
66:    or masking failure. A greedy walk of the v1.8 env shows relocation is
67:    *always* available when it could matter: 55% of zone-turns hold ≥1 idle
68:    driver, and every one of those turns has a legal relocation target (mean
69:    5.7 of 8 neighbors pass the D24 surplus test). The collapse is learned and
70:    driven by the environment's own economics: D25 prices empty travel at
71:    −0.15/km (≈ −0.75 per 5 km hop) while the immediate +1.0 dispatch credit
72:    pays *now*, so a relocation costs more than half a served order with only
73:    speculative future payoff. The training metrics show it happen gradually:
74:    `pct_relocate` decays 14.6% → 3.1% (200 it) → 0.3% (400 it) as service rate
75:    simultaneously falls 73.6% → 49.7% — the policy first strips out relocation,
76:    then loses the demand-anticipation value that relocation was supporting.
77: 
78:    **Update (same day): this is NOT MAPPO-specific and NOT slow.** IPPO on the
79:    identical env (`runs/ippo-nyc-s0-100it`) trained *healthily for ~80
80:    iterations* — 74.0% training service rate at 13.5% relocation, the best
81:    training numbers of any run — then **collapsed to the same attractor inside
82:    ~15 iterations**: iteration 81 SR 74.0% / reloc 13.3% → iteration 91 SR
83:    63.3% / reloc 4.9% → iteration 100 SR 52.8% / reloc 0.5%. Both algos, same
84:    env, same failure mode: once the policy discovers that suppressing empty
85:    travel yields an immediate reward improvement, PPO's advantage estimates
86:    push every agent off relocation and there is nothing left to stabilize the
87:    coordination value it was providing. The end state (SR 52.8%, empty 172 km)
88:    is exactly the greedy-dispatch floor.
89: 
90:    Implication for RQ2: any late-training checkpoint is a policy that has
91:    *unlearned* rebalancing, so "train longer" is anti-productive under the
92:    current reward. Options, in order of cost: (a) **early stopping** — select
93:    the checkpoint at peak *validation* service rate (this run: ~it 41–81);
94:    note the saved `checkpoint.pt` is the final iteration, so retrain with
95:    `--checkpoint-interval 5` and pick, or add best-checkpoint tracking to
96:    `train.py`; (b) reshape the relocation payoff so the delay between paying
97:    for travel and collecting the service is shorter or the travel discount
98:    smaller — this changes the reward function and therefore requires a
99:    decisions.md entry plus config version bump (AGENTS.md); (c) report the
100:    collapse itself as a finding — "both PPO variants unlearn proactive
101:    rebalancing under a pay-now/pay-later reward asymmetry" is a legitimate
102:    RQ2-adjacent result if documented with the training curves. Do not report
103:    late-training checkpoints as either algo's best effort.
104: 4. **S1 and S4 are the discriminative shifts.** Spatial permutation costs the
105:    best MAPPO 13.8 points and greedy 15.3; S4 (cold-zone concentration) is
106:    where everything collapses (LP 32.0%, MAPPO 22.8–24.1%). S2a (demand
107:    halved) is the easiest for every policy — fewer orders means less shift
108:    pressure, so it is not evidence of robustness.
109: 5. **The LP also wins the environment's own reward** (D27: S0 +17.9 vs
110:    greedy −112.7), so its lead is not an artifact of one KPI.
111: 
112: ## Caveats and blocked work
113: 
114: - **The first IPPO row exists but is not a fair comparator yet.** Both PPO
115:   variants collapse late in training (see finding 3); `ippo-it75` was selected
116:   by peak *training* SR, not validation SR, and evaluates below greedy despite
117:   a 74.4% training SR. Fair H1–H4 requires validation-selected checkpoints for
118:   both algos (train.py now writes versioned `checkpoint_it####.pt` files, but
119:   has no best-checkpoint tracking yet).
120: - **MAPPO's fair comparator is also missing.** mappo-185716 (100 it) predates
121:   the collapse and is the best RL row, but it was trained under
122:   `neighbor_relocation_only=False` (global relocation) — a different action
123:   space from the 200/400-it runs and from ippo-it75. A MAPPO run under the
124:   current v1.8 env with early stopping is needed before any IPPO-vs-MAPPO
125:   claim.
126: - All MAPPO runs are **single-seed training** (seed 0). Eval seeds vary, but
127:   training-seed variance is unmeasured.
128: - The 400-iteration pair (202335, 204712) look like duplicates by parameter
129:   probes; confirm whether they differ before reporting them as two runs.
130: - The empty-km figure spans 64–2151 km, which visually flattens the
131:   greedy/LP comparison; consider a log axis before using it in the paper.
132: - `results/shift_eval_preD27/` holds the invalid pre-D27 3-seed run (LP row is
133:   a no-op floor clone). Kept only as a safety copy; do not cite, safe to delete.
134: - `INTENT.md` still says "dispatch is a fixed rule; agents learn only
135:   `relocate` (pending D5)", but D9 superseded D5 and the env implements joint
136:   dispatch+relocate. Reconcile before writing H1–H4.
137: 
138: ## Status
139: 
140: - [x] Retrain/eval MAPPO on S0 (v1.4) — done, 4 runs.
141: - [x] Train IPPO on S0 — done (`ippo-nyc-s0-95it-ck5`); **but** both PPO
142:   variants collapse late in training, so checkpoint selection by validation SR
143:   is now the blocking item for H1–H4.
144: - [x] Tune baseline with comparable effort (D3 / D27).
145: - [x] Frozen-policy eval across S0–S6, 5 seeds → curves in `results/figures/`.
146: - [ ] Written H1–H4 answer with the curves as evidence.

1: # Demand Distribution Shift — taxonomy & protocol
2: 
3: Answers: "what exactly do we mean by demand distribution shift?" Each scenario
4: has an ID used in configs, logs, and figures. Base distribution B = the
5: training demand: NYC FHVHV train-split windows, `nyc_window_limit=500`
6: (D22 default), 50 drivers, 60-min episodes.
7: 
8: ## Shift taxonomy
9: 
10: | ID | Type | Parameterization | Real-world story |
11: |----|------|------------------|------------------|
12: | S0 | In-distribution control | B resampled (new seeds) | Sanity check: no shift |
13: | S1 | Spatial permutation | Rotate/permute origin-destination intensities across zones, strength p ∈ [0,1] | Hotspots move (new mall, office district) |
14: | S2 | Magnitude scaling | Global arrival rate × λ, λ ∈ {0.5, 0.75, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0} | Demand surge / off-season |
15: | S3 | Temporal phase shift | Peak window shifted by Δt minutes | Schedule change, daylight saving, Ramadan |
16: | S4 | Cold-zone concentration | target_zone + target_zone_pct, zone verifiably cold in train split | Policy never rewarded in target zone |
17: | S5 | Event-like spike | Short high-intensity burst at random node/time | Match ending, sudden rain |
18: | S6 | Real held-out demand | Different day/window of real trip data | The non-synthetic case |
19: 
20: Mapping to the prof's four shift families (D12): higher demand → S2;
21: spatial shift → S1 (plus S4 cold-zone concentration); temporal shift → S3;
22: event-type shift → S5. S0 is the in-distribution control.
23: 
24: ## Zone model (NYC study area)
25: 
26: 62 NYC taxi-zone centroids falling inside the Region A bbox, sorted by
27: LocationID (see `NYCConfig`, `docs/nyc-pipeline.md`). One zone-controller
28: agent per zone (D10). `BandraConfig` (synthetic grid) is kept only for
29: quick local testing.
30: 
31: ## Protocol (Fujimoto-style)
32: 
33: 1. Train on B only. Freeze policy.
34: 2. For each scenario ID and severity level: evaluate frozen policy over
35:    ≥ 5 seeds × N episodes.
36: 3. Track KPIs **over episode time**, not just final values (degradation
37:    can be gradual, not a cliff).
38: 4. Primary output: degradation curve = KPI vs shift severity, one line per
39:    method (IPPO / MAPPO / baseline / random).
40: 5. Robustness metric: area-normalized performance retention =
41:    ∫ KPI(severity) d(severity) / KPI(S0). <TODO: finalize formula after
42:    first results.>
43: 
44: ## Implemented parameterizations (Phase 4, 2026-09-14)
45: 
46: Concrete settings used in `experiments/shift_eval.py` (`nyc_window_limit=500`
47: unless noted, frozen policy, 5 episodes, `base_seed=1000`):
48: 
49: - S3 (temporal phase shift): `nyc_split="val"` — evaluation windows drawn
50:   from the validation split (different hours than the train split), simulating
51:   a shifted peak. Same order cap (500) as S0/S2c so only timing changes.
52: - S5 (event-like spike, D21 mechanism): `burst_share` of orders with
53:   request_time in [20, 30) min reassigned to `burst_zone 0`, timestamps
54:   preserved. Same total volume, time-localized (see redefined section).
55: - S6 (real held-out demand): `nyc_split="test"` — real FHVHV test-split
56:   windows never touched during training or tuning.
57: 
58: ## Redefined spatial scenarios (2026-09-14 — SUPERSEDE S1a/S1b/S4a/S5 above for reporting)
59: 
60: Audit finding: S1a/S1b/S4a/S5 were all implemented as the same static
61: `(target_zone, pct)` injection, differing only in zone + percentage —
62: the taxonomy's promised mechanisms (permutation, unseen-ness, burst
63: timing) were not implemented (S5's `event` branch was literally `pass`).
64: The old labels are deprecated but their CSVs stay on disk. New definitions:
65: 
66: - S1 (spatial permutation, strength p ∈ {0.5, 1.0}): origin AND destination
67:   zones remapped through a fixed rotation (offset = num_zones // 2,
68:   documented seed) with probability p per order; otherwise unchanged.
69:   Hotspots move instead of intensifying. Same order count, same timestamps.
70: - S4 (cold-zone concentration, k = 1): 60% of orders originate in the
71:   coldest train-demand zone (measured below — genuinely near-zero training
72:   demand). Same mechanism as static concentration (ParameterizedZoneOrderGenerator),
73:   but the target zone is verifiably unseen in training.
74: - S5 (event burst): `burst_share` of the orders with request_time inside
75:   [burst_start, burst_end] = [20, 30] min reassigned to `burst_zone 0`
76:   (centroid nodes, timestamps preserved). Same total volume, time-localized.
77:   Outside the window, demand is untouched base demand.
78: 
79: - Deprecated labels kept for backward-compat with old CSVs (all were the same
80:   static concentration-as-mechanism bug, now fixed):
81:   - S1a/S1b: static 70% concentration to zone 0 / zone 30 (not permutation).
82:   - S4a: static 60% concentration to zone 60 (not a held-out cold zone).
83:   - S5a: static 20% concentration to zone 0 (not a time burst).
84:   (Their CSVs were purged 2026-09-16 per D22/D23; definitions retained so
85:   the labels stay interpretable. The reporting labels are S1/S4/S5.)
86: - Measured cold zones (train split, winlim 500 sampling, 84 windows, 42000
87:   sampled orders, seed=42): zone 1 (44 orders, 0.105%), zone 3 (57), zone 40 (63).
88:   Hottest: zones 55/47/33 (~1563/1381/1287 each, ~3.7%/3.3%/3.1%). **S4 uses zone 1.**
89: 
90: ## Results
91: 
92: PURGED 2026-09-16 (D22): all pre-D22 eval data deleted. S2c (magnitude
93: ×1.0) stays dropped — byte-identical config to S0, pure compute waste.
94: Repopulate per `docs/shift-results.md` after v1.4 retraining.
95: 
96: ## Fair-comparison checklist
97: 
98: - [ ] Same seeds and same scenario instances across all methods.
99: - [ ] Baseline tuned on B with effort budget comparable to RL agents.
100: - [ ] Report baseline under shift too — maybe the heuristic is the robust one.
101: - [ ] Report random policy as the floor, never as the "baseline".

1: # HANDOFF.md — Current state and next steps
2: 
3: Read this file first. It tells you what exists, what's done, what's broken,
4: and what to do next. Everything else in the repo supports this.
5: Rules live in `AGENTS.md`; research scope in `INTENT.md` — this file is status, not rules.
6: 
7: ---
8: 
9: ## What this project is
10: 
11: We measure how MARL fleet-rebalancing policies (IPPO vs MAPPO) degrade under
12: passenger-demand distribution shift. This is a **measurement study**, not an
13: algorithm paper. We train on one demand distribution, freeze the policy, then
14: stress-test on shifted distributions and plot degradation curves.
15: 
16: **Advisor:** Alaa Daoud (INSA Hauts-de-France, LAMIH CNRS)
17: ---
18: 
19: ## 2026-09-16: D22 env overhaul + purge of all pre-D22 artifacts
20: 
21: - Code audit (no training logs) found six S0 blockers; fixed as config
22:   v1.4, logged in D22 (winlim 500 default, urgent-first candidates,
23:   legible demand signal, anywhere relocation, reward rescale,
24:   max_pending 80).
25: - `runs/` and `results/` purged (checkpoints, evals, figures, videos).
26:   `docs/shift-results.md` is a stub; `docs/demand-shift.md` keeps the
27:   taxonomy/mechanisms only. Do NOT mix pre/post-D22 seeds in one aggregate.
28: - Next: retrain IPPO + MAPPO on S0 (v1.4) to competence, tune baseline
29:   (D3), then frozen-policy eval S0–S6.
30: 
31: ## 2026-09-16 (later): D27 LP baseline repaired and tuned
32: 
33: - `ZoneLPRebalancer` was a silent no-op: its objective priced travel cost but
34:   put no value on covering a predicted shortfall, so with all costs non-negative
35:   the all-zero plan was exactly optimal. It matched greedy on all 10 shifts with
36:   `pct_relocate = 0.0`. No speed/horizon/threshold tuning could change that.
37: - Fixed in D27: shortfall term in the objective (`deficit_penalty_scale`),
38:   demand-rate unit bug, deterministic rounding. `speed_kmh` deliberately left at
39:   the env's true 30 km/h.
40: - Tuned on S0+S3 only (held-out shifts untouched): defaults now
41:   `lookahead_minutes=5.0`, `deficit_penalty_scale=0.4`. S0 service rate
42:   70.5% vs greedy 52.9%; S3 70.6% vs 46.3%; empty km 115 → 812.
43: - New sweep harness: `experiments/tune_lp_baseline.py` (writes to
44:   `results/lp_tuning*/`).
45: - Head-to-head re-run at 5 seeds (seed 1000–1004) over all 6 policies →
46:   `results/shift_eval/` + `results/figures/`. Numbers and caveats in
47:   `docs/shift-results.md`. The pre-D27 3-seed run is parked in
48:   `results/shift_eval_preD27/` (invalid for citation, safe to delete).
49: - Two things the re-run surfaced: the tuned LP leads every shift (S0 70.5% vs
50:   best MAPPO 57.2%), and MAPPO at 400 iterations has *stopped relocating*
51:   entirely (`pct_relocate` 0.01%, service rate down to greedy's). No IPPO
52:   checkpoints exist, so H1–H4 remain unanswerable.
53: 
54: ## 2026-09-17: validation-selection protocol bug found and fixed
55: 
56: - The first validation-selected pair (`ippo-v18-val` it12, `mappo-v18-val`
57:   it8 → `results/shift_eval_v18best/`) is NOT a clean comparison. Two bugs:
58:   (1) selection ran on the TRAINING split (same windows the policy learns
59:   on); (2) selection ran in the training env itself, so its seeded resets
60:   (9000–9002) rewrote the training RNG stream — enabling validation changed
61:   subsequent training episodes. A separate probe also confirmed the
62:   three-episode val reset replays only the first val window (cursor reset).
63: - Fixed in `train.py`: selection now runs `evaluate_deterministic` in a
64:   DEDICATED env deep-copied from the training config with `nyc_split="val"`
65:   (`_make_validation_env`); the training env is never touched by selection
66:   (toggle-verified: 2-iter training trajectories byte-identical with and
67:   without `--eval-interval`). Provenance: `TrainConfig` logs
68:   `eval_split/eval_seed/eval_episodes/eval_window_limit`; `best_checkpoint.json`
69:   records `val_split`, `eval_seed`, `eval_episodes` (auditable selection).
70:   Regression-locked in `tests/test_training_validation.py`.
71: - Consequence: **`results/shift_eval_v18best/` and the v18best rows must not
72:   be merged into the headline artifacts or cited as validation-selected.**
73:   Treat as exploratory only. Next: retrain IPPO + MAPPO on S0 with
74:   `--eval-interval` (now on the true val split), then rerun the 5-seed
75:   shift eval for the fair H1–H4 comparison.
76: ---
77: 
78: ## What exists right now (verified working)
79: 
80: ### Simulation (RideGym)
81: - Bandra West, Mumbai — OSMnx graph at `data/bandra_west.gpickle`
82: - `main.py` — `--policy random|heuristic`, config-driven (`BandraConfig`),
83:   saves MP4 or opens interactive viewer
84: - `src/marl_fleet_rebalancing/visualization.py` — custom MP4 renderer + interactive viewer
85: - `src/marl_fleet_rebalancing/baselines/heuristic_baseline.py` — LP-based
86:   rolling-horizon rebalancer (MIVR-lite, D3); raw RideGym interface, needs a
87:   wrapper port for fair comparison (Phase 3)
88: 
89: ### PettingZoo wrapper (zone-controller, D10 — reworked 2026-09-08)
90: - `src/marl_fleet_rebalancing/env/pettingzoo_env.py` — AEC wrapper, ONE AGENT
91:   PER ZONE (`zone_i`); joint dispatch+rebalance action space per D9; per-zone
92:   idle-driver FIFO resolution (D13). Smoke test passes on Bandra.
93: - `src/marl_fleet_rebalancing/env/test_wrapper.py` — smoke test; `--nyc` flag
94:   runs the NYC split mode (62 taxi-zone agents, real FHVHV window). Passes.
95: - NYC mode: `order_source="nyc"` (NYCConfig) replays split windows via
96:   `MultiWindowNYCOrderGenerator`; zone model = taxi-zone centroids; verified
97:   600 turns on a train window.
98: - Tested: Bandra 25 zones × 300 turns; NYC 62 zones × 600 turns.
99: 
100: ### Config
101: - `src/marl_fleet_rebalancing/config.py` — `EnvConfig`, `NYCConfig`, `BandraConfig`
102: - All parameters documented with source/rationale. No magic numbers.
103: 
104: ### Reward function & Credit Assignment
105: - Implemented in `pettingzoo_env.py` (`_compute_zone_rewards` method).
106: - Formula per zone: $R_z = (1 - \text{team\_share}) \cdot R_{\text{local}, z} + \text{team\_share} \cdot \bar{R}_{\text{global}}$.
107: - Local credit assignment: $+5.0$ to the zone that dispatched the completed order, $-0.1 \times \text{km}$ to the zone commanding empty movement (exact physical distance from RideGym events), and $-1.0 \times \text{wait}$ localized by order origin.
108: - Default weights: $\alpha = 1.0, \beta = 0.1, \gamma = 5.0, \text{team\_share} = 0.2$.
109: 
110: ### Docs
111: - `docs/agent-mechanics.md` — Diagnostics of root issues, what agents observe, action mapping, and credit assignment.
112: - `docs/decisions.md` — D1-D8 decisions logged
113: - `docs/demand-shift.md` — S0-S6 shift taxonomy
114: - `docs/literature.md` — gap sentence + adjacent work table
115: - `docs/pettingzoo-design-decisions.md` — why AEC, shuffled order, action mask as method
116: - `docs/pettingzoo-wrapper-plan.md` — implementation plan
117: - `docs/nyc-pipeline.md` — NYC data setup steps
118: - `docs/architecture.md` — module map + KPI definitions

1: # Architecture
2: 
3: ## Current (verified against code)
4: 
5: ```
6: main.py                              # smoke test: --policy random|heuristic, MP4/interactive render
7: build_zone_partition.py              # one-time script: generates data/zone_partition.json
8: marl_fleet_rebalancing/
9:   agents/
10:     random_policy.py                 # random_actions(env, obs)
11:     ippo.py                          # IPPO learner: masked PPO-clip + GAE,
12:                                      # decentralized critic, shared params
13:     mappo.py                         # MAPPO learner: shared actor + centralized
14:                                      # critic over global state (D4)
15:   baselines/
16:     greedy_baseline.py               # greedy nearest-idle dispatch floor, zero relocation
17:     lp_baseline.py                   # rolling-horizon LP rebalancer on the wrapper env
18:                                      # (D3; repaired + tuned in D27)
19:     heuristic_baseline.py            # earlier LP rebalancer on the raw RideGym dict
20:                                      # interface; superseded by lp_baseline.py
21:   config.py                          # EnvConfig / NYCConfig / BandraConfig (D7)
22:   demand/
23:     zones.py                         # ZonePartition + get_default()
24:     generators.py                    # synthetic / NYC-window / dataframe order sources
25:   env/
26:     pettingzoo_env.py                # RideGymAEC wrapper: zone-controller agents
27:                                      # (D10/D13), joint dispatch+relocate (D9),
28:                                      # NYC split mode (order_source="nyc")
29:     test_wrapper.py                  # wrapper smoke test (Bandra; --nyc for NYC)
30:   experiments/
31:     train.py                         # IPPO/MAPPO training entry (--algo);
32:                                      # logs config/seed/git/scenario/KPIs
33:                                      # to runs/ (git-ignored)
34:     evaluate.py                      # frozen policy multi-seed eval & MP4/interactive viewer
35:     shift_eval.py                    # frozen-policy eval across S0-S6 -> results/shift_eval/
36:     plot_shifts.py                   # degradation curves from summary.json -> results/figures/
37:     tune_lp_baseline.py              # LP baseline hyperparameter sweep (train/val shifts only)
38:   networks/
39:     encoder.py                       # obs flattening + ZoneActorCritic MLP
40:                                      # + CentralizedCritic (MAPPO value head)
41:   visualization.py                   # custom MP4 renderer + interactive viewer
42: references/                          # diagnosis notes (e.g. lp-rebalancer-zero-transfers.md)
43: data/
44:   bandra_west.gpickle                # OSMnx road network (git-ignored)
45:   zone_partition.json                # 12-zone 3x4 mapping, verified 1464/1464 nodes
46:   nyc/                               # NYC pipeline output (git-ignored):
47:                                      # zone_centroids.csv, manhattan.gpickle
48:                                      # (4868 nodes), splits/ 84 train / 12 val / 12 test
49: ```
50: 
51: Still empty (only `__init__.py`):
52: - `shifts/` — planned for demand shift generators
53: 
54: Populated since: `agents/` (ippo.py, mappo.py), `networks/` (encoder.py),
55: `experiments/` (train.py, evaluate.py, shift_eval.py, plot_shifts.py,
56: tune_lp_baseline.py), `baselines/` (greedy, lp).
57: 
58: ### RideGym interface contract (do not break)
59: 
60: - Env: `RidePoolEnv(num_drivers, driver_capacity, dt, horizon, order_timeout,
61:   order_generator, road_network)`.
62: - Per-vehicle action is a dict, keys mutually exclusive:
63:   - `{"orders": [order_id, ...]}` — bid on pending orders
64:   - `{"relocate": index_or_coord}` — idle vehicles only; THIS is the
65:     rebalancing action our agents will learn.
66: - obs/rewards are dicts keyed by vehicle id; `dones["__all__"]` ends the episode.
67: - Conflicting bids on the same order raise `ConflictError` — any multi-agent
68:   dispatch policy MUST coordinate bids (central dispatcher or conflict-free
69:   bidding rule). This is the main integration risk for MARL.
70: - Order sources are swappable: the wrapper selects via `EnvConfig.order_source`
71:   — `synthetic` (OSMnxOrderGenerator), `nyc` (MultiWindowNYCOrderGenerator over
72:   data/nyc/splits, one window per episode; train random / val+test cyclic),
73:   `dataframe` (DataFrameOrderGenerator; endpoints must be on-network). Demand
74:   shift is injected by swapping/parameterizing the generator, never by editing
75:   env internals.
76: - The wrapper's region model (one zone per agent) always lives in the network's
77:   real coordinate space: NYC taxi-zone centroids, or a `relocation_grid` over
78:   the network bounds for synthetic maps (D13).
79: 
80: ### Not yet built
81: 
82: - `metrics.py` — KPIs currently come from `env.kpi_summary()` and the eval
83:   loops in `experiments/`, not from a shared metrics module.
84: - `demand/scenarios.py` — shift mechanisms live in `config.py` (`demand_shift_mode`
85:   and friends) and the `SHIFTS` table in `experiments/shift_eval.py`.
86: - `shifts/` — still empty (`__init__.py` only); shift injection is config-driven.
87: - `experiments/configs/` — no per-run config files; runs record their own
88:   manifest under `runs/<name>/manifest.json`.
89: 
90: ### Zone partition (for demand shift evaluation)
91: 
92: - Bandra West OSMnx graph (1464 nodes, 3281 edges) partitioned into 12 zones
93:   via 3×4 spatial grid
94: - Zone mapping persisted as `data/zone_partition.json` (node_id → zone_id)
95: - Order generators reference zones rather than individual graph nodes for
96:   demand injection, ensuring shifts operate at the zone level
97: 
98: ## KPI definitions (single source of truth)
99: 
100: - **Service rate** = completed / total orders.
101: - **Cancellation rate** = cancelled / total orders (timeout = 2.0 min currently).
102: - **Avg wait time** = pickup_time - request_time, over completed orders.
103: - **Empty VMT** = distance driven without passengers (incl. rebalancing legs).
104: - **Fleet utilization** = 1 − (idle vehicle-time / total vehicle-time), averaged
105:   over the episode. (Brief metric; add when metrics module is built.)
106: - **Rebalancing trips** = count of `relocate` actions executed.
107: - All KPIs computed per episode, then aggregated mean ± std over seeds.

1: """IPPO: Independent PPO for the zone-controller fleet-rebalancing env.
2: 
3: All zone agents share one actor-critic network (parameter sharing over
4: homogeneous agents) but learn independently: the critic is decentralized
5: (own observation only) and the reward is the shared global task reward.
6: This is the IPPO baseline of the study (D4) — MAPPO replaces the critic's
7: input with the centralized state (Phase 2 step 2).
8: """

48: class RolloutBuffer:
49:     """Fixed-size buffer of per-agent transitions for one PPO update.
50: 
51:     Transition (agent_slot, t) = (obs, action, logp, value, reward, done,
52:     action_mask). Rewards/dones are filled at the agent's NEXT selection
53:     (standard AEC delayed-close); GAE runs per agent slot.
54: 
55:     MAPPO mode (``state_dim`` given): each transition additionally stores
56:     the centralized global state, consumed by the centralized critic.
57:     IPPO paths (``state_dim=None``) are untouched.

61:         self,
62:         num_agents: int,

79:         self.rewards = np.zeros((num_agents, num_turns), dtype=np.float32)
80:         self.dones = np.zeros((num_agents, num_turns), dtype=np.float32)

1: """Neural network architectures for the MARL policies.
2: 
3: Zone-controller observation encoder + shared actor-critic used by IPPO and
4: MAPPO. One actor network is shared across all zone agents (parameter
5: sharing) — zone identity enters through ``zone_location``, per D13. MAPPO
6: keeps the shared actor and replaces the decentralized critic head with
7: :class:`CentralizedCritic` over the global state.
8: """
9: 
10: from __future__ import annotations
11: 
12: import numpy as np
13: import torch
14: import torch.nn as nn
15: from gymnasium.spaces import Dict
16: 
17: 
18: def obs_to_vector(obs: dict[str, np.ndarray]) -> np.ndarray:
19:     """Flatten a zone-controller observation dict into a fixed-order vector.
20: 
21:     Key order must match the encoder's expected input layout; it is derived
22:     from the wrapper's observation space (see pettingzoo_env.py / D13/D23).
23:     """
24:     return np.concatenate(
25:         [
26:             obs["zone_location"].ravel(),
27:             obs["zone_state"].ravel(),
28:             obs["all_zone_state"].ravel(),
29:             obs["pending_orders"].ravel(),
30:             obs["pending_mask"].ravel(),
31:             obs["pending_passengers"].ravel(),
32:             obs["time_frac"].ravel(),
33:         ]
34:     ).astype(np.float32)
35: 
36: 
37: def obs_dim(obs_space: Dict) -> int:
38:     """Flat observation dimension from the wrapper's Dict observation space."""
39:     total = 0
40:     for key in (
41:         "zone_location", "zone_state", "all_zone_state", "pending_orders",
42:         "pending_mask", "pending_passengers", "time_frac",
43:     ):
44:         total += int(np.prod(obs_space[key].shape))
45:     return total
46: 
47: 
48: class ZoneActorCritic(nn.Module):
49:     """Shared actor-critic MLP over flattened zone-controller observations.
50: 
51:     Actor head outputs logits over the joint dispatch+rebalance action space
52:     (D9); the caller masks illegal actions before sampling. Critic head is the
53:     decentralized value estimate for IPPO (own observation only).
54:     """
55: 
56:     def __init__(
57:         self,
58:         observation_dim: int,
59:         action_dim: int,
60:         hidden_dim: int = 256,
61:     ) -> None:
62:         super().__init__()
63:         self.trunk = nn.Sequential(
64:             nn.Linear(observation_dim, hidden_dim),
65:             nn.Tanh(),
66:             nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),
67:             nn.Tanh(),
68:         )
69:         self.actor = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, action_dim)
70:         self.critic = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)
71: 
72:     def forward(
73:         self, obs: torch.Tensor, action_mask: torch.Tensor | None = None,
74:     ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
75:         """Return (masked action logits, value estimates)."""
76:         h = self.trunk(obs)
77:         logits = self.actor(h)
78:         if action_mask is not None:
79:             logits = logits.masked_fill(~action_mask, float("-inf"))
80:         return logits, self.critic(h).squeeze(-1)
81: 
82:     @torch.no_grad()
83:     def act(
84:         self,
85:         obs: torch.Tensor,
86:         action_mask: torch.Tensor,
87:         deterministic: bool = False,
88:     ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
89:         """Sample actions; returns (action, log_prob, value)."""
90:         logits, value = self.forward(obs, action_mask)
91:         dist = torch.distributions.Categorical(logits=logits)
92:         action = dist.mode if deterministic else dist.sample()
93:         return action, dist.log_prob(action), value
94: 
95: 
96: class CentralizedCritic(nn.Module):
97:     """Global-state value MLP for MAPPO's centralized critic (D4).
98: 
99:     Same trunk architecture as the actor (Linear-Tanh x2) but over the
100:     wrapper's centralized ``state()`` (all-zone fleet state + pending
101:     features + clock) instead of a single zone's observation.
102:     """
103: 
104:     def __init__(
105:         self,
106:         state_dim: int,
107:         hidden_dim: int = 256,
108:     ) -> None:
109:         super().__init__()
110:         self.trunk = nn.Sequential(
111:             nn.Linear(state_dim, hidden_dim),
112:             nn.Tanh(),
113:             nn.Linear(hidden_dim, hidden_dim),
114:             nn.Tanh(),
115:         )
116:         self.value = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, 1)
117: 
118:     def forward(self, state: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
119:         """Return value estimates for a batch of global states."""
120:         return self.value(self.trunk(state)).squeeze(-1)

460:   2. **Training Horizon Scale-Up:** Scale training to 200 iterations (1.2M transitions) to observe deep convergence properties and long-term rebalancing policy dynamics.
461: 
462: ## D27 — LP rebalancer objective repair (silent no-op → tuned baseline)
463: - Date: 2026-09-16, Status: accepted
464: - Problem: `ZoneLPRebalancer` was indistinguishable from `GreedyZoneDispatcher`
465:   on all 10 shift scenarios (`pct_relocate = 0.0`, byte-identical KPIs). Root
466:   cause is structural, not a tuning accident: the LP minimised travel cost
467:   subject only to upper bounds (`outflow <= surplus`, `inflow <= deficit`,
468:   `total <= n_idle`), so with all `c_ij >= 0` the all-zero plan was feasible
469:   *and* optimal. The solver was correct; it was always right to move nothing.
470:   No `speed_kmh`, `min_idle_to_rebalance` or `lookahead_minutes` value can fix
471:   that (verified: even cost scale 0.01 returns zeros).
472: - Decisions (`baselines/lp_baseline.py`, baseline-local; no env dynamics,
473:   reward, or observation/action-space change):
474:   1. **Shortfall term in the objective.** Minimise
475:      `sum c_ij x_ij - lambda * sum_j inflow_j`, with
476:      `lambda = deficit_penalty_scale * mean(off-diagonal c)`. The reduced cost
477:      `c_ij - lambda` is the net value of a relocation, so a route is used only
478:      when filling the destination's predicted shortfall beats its travel time.
479:      `deficit_penalty_scale = 0` reproduces the old behaviour exactly.
480:   2. **Self-transfer variables pinned to zero** (`bounds = (0, 0)`) instead of
481:      relying on a zero diagonal cost.
482:   3. **Demand-rate units fixed.** `env._pending_per_zone` is pending orders per
483:      zone divided by fleet size (D22), but the estimator multiplied it by the
484:      total pending count; now scaled by `num_drivers`.
485:   4. **Deterministic rounding.** Fractional LP flows were rounded against the
486:      global `np.random` stream; the policy now owns a generator seeded from
487:      `reset(seed)`.
488:   5. **`speed_kmh` left at the environment's true value (30 km/h).** The
489:      previous reference note told readers to *lower* it, which is backwards
490:      (cost is travel time) and would be fudged physics in the other direction.
491:   6. **Defaults tuned on S0 (train) + S3 (val) only:** `lookahead_minutes 3 → 5`,
492:      `deficit_penalty_scale 1.0 → 0.4`. S1/S4/S5/S6 stay held out (AGENTS.md
493:      rule 3).
494: - Result (5 seeds, base_seed 1000, greedy floor in brackets): S0 service rate
495:   70.5% [52.9%], S3 70.6% [46.3%], `pct_relocate` 7.2% [0.0%]. Empty distance
496:   rises 115 km → 812 km, i.e. the gain is bought with 5–7× the repositioning
497:   distance; both KPIs must be reported together.
498: - Notably the *shorter* horizon won: a horizon longer than the mean inter-zone
499:   travel time (8.6 min) flags ~50 of 62 zones as short and destroys the spatial
500:   selectivity of the plan, so 15–30 min scored worse than 5 min on both service
501:   rate and empty km.
502: - The extra distance is not wasted: the tuned LP also beats greedy on the
503:   environment's own reward (S0 +17.9 vs -112.7), so the gain is not an artifact
504:   of optimising a single KPI.
505: - Held-out check (scored once, after defaults were frozen): LP >= greedy on
506:   service rate in all of S1/S2a-e/S4/S5/S6, mean retention 90.5% vs 85.7%,
507:   with the largest margin on S1 (79.4% vs 71.1%). Full KPIs in
508:   `results/shift_eval_lp_fixed/`.
509: - Consequence: LP is now a genuinely tuned baseline (D3) rather than a floor
510:   clone. Pre-D27 LP rows in `results/shift_eval/` are invalid and must not be
511:   cited; re-run baselines before any head-to-head.
512: 
513: ## D28 — Relocation arrival bonus & NYC action-space compression
514: - Date: 2026-09-17, Status: accepted
515: - Problem: Agents never learn relocation because the credit arrives too late
516:   (8-18 turns after the command) and is too small relative to the immediate
517:   empty-km cost. With gamma=0.99, the discounted value of +0.3 assist credit
518:   perceived 12 turns early is ~0.266, while the immediate cost of a 2 km
519:   relocation is -0.30 — net negative. Additionally, max_pending=80 wastes
520:   50+ dispatch slots that are always masked, diluting relocation actions in
521:   the policy output (62 out of 143 vs 62 out of 93).
522: - Decisions:
523:   1. **Relocation arrival bonus** (`prospective_bonus_scale`): When a relocated
524:      driver arrives at its destination zone (status transitions from
525:      "relocating" to non-"relocating"), the relocator zone receives an
526:      immediate bonus: `prospective_bonus_scale * pending_demand_at_dest / num_drivers`.
527:      This eliminates the temporal credit delay — the relocator is rewarded at
528:      arrival, not at eventual dispatch. Default 0.0 (disabled); NYCConfig set
529:      to 2.0.
530:   2. **NYC max_pending 80 → 30**: Measured peak concurrent pending is ~28.
531:      Reduces action space from 143 to 93, observation from ~746 to ~506
532:      features, and makes relocation actions proportionally more likely to be
533:      sampled (62/93 vs 62/143).
534:   3. **Config version bump**: 1.8 → 1.9.
535: - Implementation:
536:   - `pettingzoo_env.py`: Detect arrivals in `_flush_turn()` by comparing
537:     old/new driver statuses before/after `env.step()`. Store in
538:     `_relocator_arrivals`. Apply bonus in `_compute_zone_rewards()` section 1b.
539:   - `config.py`: Added `prospective_bonus_scale: float = 0.0` to `EnvConfig`;
540:     NYCConfig sets it to 2.0.
541: - Revisit if:
542:   - Agents over-relocate (high empty km, low service rate) → reduce
543:     prospective_bonus_scale to 1.0.
544:   - Agents still don't relocate → increase to 3.0-5.0 or add a direct
545:     relocation success bonus when the relocated driver serves an order.
546:   - The entropy_coef change (D19 recommends 0.01 alongside reward shaping
547:     changes) needs retuning — use 0.05 for initial exploration, anneal to
548:     0.01 after 50 iterations.
549: - Note: D19 concluded "the binding constraint is reward shaping, not
550:   exploration." That holds for the old broken reward. With the arrival bonus
551:   fixed, exploration of relocation actions becomes the next bottleneck.
552:   Recommend `--entropy-coef 0.05` for the first 50 iterations, then anneal
553:   to 0.01. This is a judgment call to be validated on train/val only.
554:

1: """PettingZoo AEC wrapper around RideGym at ZONE-CONTROLLER granularity (D10).
2: 
3: One agent per relocation zone (taxi zone for NYC, grid cell for synthetic
4: maps). Each zone controller observes zone-level state and issues one action
5: per turn:
6: 
7:     0                              -> no zone action
8:     1 .. max_pending               -> dispatch pending order (i-1) to an idle
9:                                       driver currently in this zone
10:     max_pending+1 .. +num_zones    -> relocate one idle driver of this zone
11:                                       to zone (action - max_pending - 1)
12: 
13: At turn flush the wrapper resolves each zone action to a concrete RideGym
14: driver: per-zone FIFO queues of idle drivers (rebuilt from the last RideGym
15: observation), consumed in the agents' shuffled first-come-first-served order
16: (seeded RNG). Drivers never selected issue no action and keep their current
17: state. This keeps the D9 joint dispatch+rebalancing action space while the
18: decision granularity is zones, per D10.
19: 
20: All numeric constants come from EnvConfig — no magic numbers here.
21: Design decisions are documented in docs/pettingzoo-design-decisions.md and
22: docs/decisions.md (D9, D10, D13).
23: """

209:         scenario += f"-target-zone-{env_cfg.target_zone}"
210:     env = RideGymAEC(config=env_cfg, seed=seed)
211:     return env, env_cfg, scenario

214: def collect_rollout(
215:     env: RideGymAEC,
216:     learner: IPPO | MAPPO,
217:     buffer: RolloutBuffer,
218:     total_turns: int,
219:     use_state: bool = False,
220: ) -> dict:
221:     """Collect agent transitions for ``total_turns`` env turns (AEC pattern).
222: 
223:     **Optimized**: all zone agents are batched into a single forward pass
224:     per turn instead of calling ``learner.act()`` once per zone. This keeps
225:     the GPU busy processing batch_size=N_zones instead of batch_size=1.
226: 
227:     Rewards close at the agent's next selection (PettingZoo delayed-close
228:     semantics): the reward read for an agent is the one produced by the env
229:     step that followed its previous action. Rollouts must end exactly at
230:     episode boundaries (total_turns = k * episode_turns), so no value
231:     bootstrap is needed. Returns episode KPIs.
232: 
233:     With ``use_state`` (MAPPO), each transition also stores the centralized
234:     global state and values come from the centralized critic; otherwise
235:     values are decentralized (IPPO).
236: 
237:     Episode accounting: when the AEC termination dance empties ``env.agents``,
238:     the ended episode's KPIs are read from the env BEFORE the next reset.
239:     """
240:     if total_turns <= 0:
241:         raise ValueError(f"collect_rollout requires total_turns > 0, got {total_turns}")
242:     num_zones = env._num_zones
243:     counts = np.zeros(num_zones, dtype=int)
244:     pending: dict[str, tuple[int, int]] = {}
245:     episodes = 0
246:     served_total = 0
247:     cancelled_total = 0
248:     empty_km_total = 0.0
249:     wait_times_all: list[float] = []
250:     acts_idle = 0
251:     acts_dispatch = 0
252:     acts_relocate = 0
253:     episode_ended = False
254: 
255:     # Per-agent observation/mask buffers for batch collection.
256:     # Each slot is a single observation vector (obs_dim,) not a full
257:     # episode rollout, so allocate (num_zones, obs_dim).
258:     _obs_dim = obs_dim  # for the closure below
259:     obs_batch = np.empty(
260:         (num_zones, buffer.obs.shape[2]), dtype=np.float32,
261:     )
262:     mask_batch = np.empty(
263:         (num_zones, buffer.action_mask.shape[2]), dtype=bool,
264:     )
265:     # MAPPO centralized-state batch (D18 fix): per-agent state = global
266:     # state + own zone_location, matching train.py's state_dim. IPPO leaves
267:     # this None and uses decentralized actor values.
268:     if use_state:
269:         assert buffer.states is not None, (
270:             "use_state=True requires a RolloutBuffer with state_dim (MAPPO)."
271:         )
272:         state_batch: np.ndarray | None = np.empty(
273:             (num_zones, buffer.states.shape[2]), dtype=np.float32,
274:         )
275:     else:
276:         state_batch = None
277: 
278:     while counts.min() < total_turns or env.agents:
279:         if not env.agents:
280:             if episode_ended:
281:                 kpi = env.kpi_summary()
282:                 served_total += int(kpi["served"])
283:                 cancelled_total += int(kpi["cancelled"])
284:                 empty_km_total += float(kpi["empty_km"])
285:                 if kpi["avg_wait"] > 0:
286:                     wait_times_all.append(kpi["avg_wait"])
287:                 episodes += 1
288:                 episode_ended = False
289:             if counts.min() < total_turns:
290:                 env.reset(seed=None)
291:             continue
292: 
293:         # Selection-ordered turn (AEC correctness): env.step() records the
294:         # action for the CURRENTLY SELECTED agent, so a turn's 25 steps must
295:         # walk env._agent_order exactly. The pre-batching loop stepped
296:         # agent_selection directly; batching must preserve that walk, or zone
297:         # actions silently execute on the wrong zones (found by audit).
298:         sel_order = [int(z) for z in env._agent_order]
299: 
300:         if not any(counts[s] < total_turns for s in sel_order):
301:             # All turns collected but agents remain: drain without acting.
302:             # The episode's KPIs are accounted BEFORE the drain flush below
303:             # (an extra RideGym step) can pollute them. Pending delayed
304:             # rewards are closed from the per-agent reward tables directly.
305:             if not episode_ended:
306:                 kpi = env.kpi_summary()
307:                 served_total += int(kpi["served"])
308:                 cancelled_total += int(kpi["cancelled"])
309:                 empty_km_total += float(kpi["empty_km"])
310:                 if kpi["avg_wait"] > 0:
311:                     wait_times_all.append(kpi["avg_wait"])
312:                 episodes += 1
313:             for agent in list(env.agents):
314:                 if agent in pending:
315:                     s_slot, s_turn = pending.pop(agent)
316:                     buffer.close(
317:                         s_slot, s_turn,
318:                         float(env.rewards.get(agent, 0.0)),
319:                         done=bool(
320:                             env.terminations.get(agent, False)
321:                             or env.truncations.get(agent, False)
322:                         ),
323:                     )
324:                 env.step(None)
325:             episode_ended = False
326:             continue
327: 
328:         # Phase 1: close delayed rewards + observe. No env steps here, so the
329:         # Per-agent tables are read directly (D18 fix): env.last() reports
330:         # only the currently-selected agent, so calling it per zone
331:         # misattributes rewards and done-flags. Skipping the second
332:         # env.last() build also removes a redundant observation per agent.
333:         # MAPPO (use_state): values come from the centralized critic over
334:         # per-agent global states; the actor head's decentralized values are
335:         # discarded, per MAPPO.act's documented contract.
336:         g_state = env.state() if use_state else None
337:         dead: set[int] = set()
338:         live_idx: list[int] = []
339:         for i, slot in enumerate(sel_order):
340:             agent = f"zone_{slot}"
341:             if agent in pending:
342:                 s_slot, s_turn = pending.pop(agent)
343:                 buffer.close(
344:                     s_slot, s_turn,
345:                     float(env.rewards.get(agent, 0.0)),
346:                     done=bool(
347:                         env.terminations.get(agent, False)
348:                         or env.truncations.get(agent, False)
349:                     ),
350:                 )
351:             if (
352:                 counts[slot] >= total_turns
353:                 or env.terminations.get(agent, False)
354:                 or env.truncations.get(agent, False)
355:             ):
356:                 if env.terminations.get(agent, False) or env.truncations.get(agent, False):
357:                     episode_ended = True
358:                     dead.add(slot)
359:                 continue
360:             obs_dict = env.observe(agent)
361:             obs_batch[i] = obs_to_vector(obs_dict)
362:             mask_batch[i] = env.action_mask(agent)
363:             if use_state:
364:                 assert g_state is not None and state_batch is not None
365:                 state_batch[i] = np.concatenate(
366:                     [g_state, np.asarray(
367:                         obs_dict["zone_location"].ravel(), dtype=np.float32,
368:                     )],
369:                 )
370:             live_idx.append(i)
371: 
372:         # Single batched forward pass for the live zones (if any).
373:         # act_of maps zone slot -> (batch position, action, logprob, value).
374:         act_of: dict[int, tuple[int, int, float, float]] = {}
375:         if live_idx:
376:             _act_t, _logp_t, _val_t = learner.act(
377:                 obs_batch[live_idx], mask_batch[live_idx],
378:             )
379:             act_values = _act_t.astype(np.int64)

367:         - Pending-order features (sorted most-urgent-first, capped to
368:           max_pending) and per-zone counts of pending orders by origin.
369:         - Per-zone fleet state: fraction of the fleet in the zone and the idle

372:         cfg = self._cfg

375:         # --- Pending orders: most urgent (longest waiting) first ------------

379:         # 1 + num_zones times via per-order Python calls, and each zone ran

381:         #   global: waiting_time desc (stable),
382:         #   per-zone: distance asc, waiting_time desc (stable).
383:         # np.argsort/lexsort with kind="stable" reproduce Python's stable
384:         # sort order exactly (identical floats: same IEEE op order).
385:         capacity = max(cfg.driver_capacity, 1)
386:         timeout = max(cfg.order_timeout, 1e-9)

390:         self._zone_pending_features[:] = 0.0

568:     def action_mask(self, agent: str) -> np.ndarray:
569:         mask = np.zeros(self._action_dim, dtype=bool)
570:         mask[0] = True  # no zone action is always valid
571:         if self._last_obs is None:
572:             return mask
573:         zone_idx = self._zone_int_id(agent)
574:         # Dispatch orders: valid if there is at least one dispatchable driver (idle or relocating, D14)
575:         has_dispatchable = bool(getattr(self, "_zone_dispatchable_counts", self._zone_idle_counts)[zone_idx] > 0)
576:         if has_dispatchable:
577:             candidates = self._zone_candidate_orders[zone_idx] if zone_idx < len(self._zone_candidate_orders) else []
578:             for i, cand in enumerate(candidates[: self._max_pending]):
579:                 # Intra-turn collision masking (D15): mask order if already claimed this turn
580:                 if cand["order_id"] not in getattr(self, "_claimed_orders_this_turn", set()):
581:                     mask[1 + i] = True
582: 
583:         # Relocate actions: only valid if there is a purely IDLE driver in this zone (D14)
584:         has_idle = bool(self._zone_idle_counts[zone_idx] > 0)
585:         if has_idle:
586:             neighbor_only = getattr(self._cfg, "neighbor_relocation_only", False)
587:             neighbours = self.env.region_neighbours[zone_idx] if (neighbor_only and zone_idx < len(self.env.region_neighbours)) else range(self._num_zones)
588:             for j in neighbours:
589:                 if j != zone_idx and 0 <= j < self._num_zones:
590:                     # Surplus-target relocation masking (D24):
591:                     # Mask out target zone j if it already holds an idle vehicle surplus
592:                     idle_j = int(self._zone_idle_counts[j])
593:                     pending_j = int(np.round(self._pending_per_zone[j] * max(self._num_drivers, 1)))
594:                     if idle_j <= max(pending_j, 1):
595:                         mask[1 + self._max_pending + j] = True
596:         return mask

598:     # ---------------------------------------------------------------- actions
599: 
600:     def _decode_action(self, action: int, zone_idx: int = 0) -> dict | None:
601:         if action is None or action == 0:
602:             return None
603:         if 1 <= action <= self._max_pending:
604:             idx = action - 1
605:             if 0 <= zone_idx < len(self._zone_candidate_orders):
606:                 candidates = self._zone_candidate_orders[zone_idx]
607:                 if idx < len(candidates):
608:                     return {"orders": [candidates[idx]["order_id"]]}
609:             elif idx < len(self._current_pending):
610:                 return {"orders": [self._current_pending[idx]["order_id"]]}
611:             return None
612:         target_zone = action - self._max_pending - 1
613:         if 0 <= target_zone < self._num_zones:
614:             return {"relocate": int(target_zone)}
615:         return None
616: 
617:     def _advance_selection(self) -> None:
618:         if not self.agents:
619:             return
620:         if not self._turn_complete:
621:             zone_idx = self._zone_int_id(self.agent_selection)
622:             pos = int(np.where(self._agent_order == zone_idx)[0][0])
623:             if pos + 1 < self._num_zones:
624:                 next_zone_idx = int(self._agent_order[pos + 1])
625:                 self.agent_selection = f"zone_{next_zone_idx}"
626:                 return
627:         self._turn_complete = True
628:         self._flush_turn()

630:     def _compute_zone_rewards(
631:         self,
632:         events: dict[int, dict],
633:         dispatched_this_turn: list[tuple[int, int, int]] | None = None,
634:     ) -> np.ndarray:
635:         """Compute localized credit assignment rewards per zone controller.
636: 
637:         R_z = (1 - team_share) * R_local,z + team_share * R_global_mean
638: 
639:         Local credit assignment (D23):
640:         1. Served demand (+gamma): credited IMMEDIATELY to the zone controller that
641:            dispatched the order at this turn (with rebalancing assist sharing).
642:            Zero temporal credit delay.
643:         2. Empty deadhead distance penalty (-beta): charged directly to the zone
644:            controller that commanded the driver (relocations shared 50/50).
645:         3. Pending waiting time penalty (-alpha): charged to the zone containing
646:            the pending order's origin (nearest zone centroid).
647:         4. Cancellation penalty (-cancel_penalty): shared between origin zone and
648:            neighboring zones holding idle cars.
649:         """
650:         from ride_gym.enums import OrderStatus
651: 
652:         cfg = self._cfg
653:         local_rewards = np.zeros(self._num_zones, dtype=np.float32)
654:         cents = np.asarray(self.env.relocation_points, dtype=float)
655: 
656:         self._prev_served = sum(
657:             1 for o in self.env.orders.values()
658:             if o.status == OrderStatus.COMPLETED
659:         )
660: 
661:         # 1. Immediate served demand attribution (+gamma) with rebalancing assist sharing (D23)
662:         if dispatched_this_turn:
663:             for oid, did, dispatcher in dispatched_this_turn:
664:                 relocator = self._driver_relocator.get(did)
665:                 if dispatcher is not None and 0 <= dispatcher < self._num_zones:
666:                     if relocator is not None and relocator != dispatcher and 0 <= relocator < self._num_zones:
667:                         # Rebalancing assist credit: 70% to local dispatcher, 30% to the zone that supplied the vehicle
668:                         local_rewards[dispatcher] += 0.7 * cfg.gamma
669:                         local_rewards[relocator] += 0.3 * cfg.gamma
670:                     else:
671:                         local_rewards[dispatcher] += cfg.gamma
672:                 self._driver_relocator.pop(did, None)
673: 
674:         # 1b. Relocation arrival bonus (D28: immediate credit when relocated driver arrives)
675:         # Eliminates the 8-18 turn delay where the relocator zone had to wait
676:         # for the driver to be dispatched by the destination zone.
677:         # Bonus = prospective_bonus_scale * pending_demand_at_destination / num_drivers
678:         prospective_bonus = float(getattr(cfg, "prospective_bonus_scale", 0.0))
679:         if prospective_bonus > 0.0:
680:             for did in getattr(self, "_relocator_arrivals", []):
681:                 relocator = self._driver_relocator.get(did)
682:                 if relocator is not None and 0 <= relocator < self._num_zones:
683:                     # Driver is now at destination; current_region is the target zone
684:                     d = self._last_obs[did]["self"]
685:                     dest_zone = int(d.get("current_region", 0))
686:                     dest_zone = max(0, min(dest_zone, self._num_zones - 1))
687:                     pending_at_dest = float(self._pending_per_zone[dest_zone])
688:                     bonus = prospective_bonus * pending_at_dest
689:                     local_rewards[relocator] += bonus
690: 
691:         # 2. Empty deadhead distance penalty (-beta) with shared relocation cost
692:         for did, event in events.items():
693:             if event.get("is_empty_move", False):
694:                 empty_m = float(event.get("distance_moved", 0.0))
695:                 self._episode_empty_m += empty_m
696:                 empty_km = empty_m / _M_PER_KM / max(cfg.empty_distance_norm_km, 1e-9)
697:                 commander = self._driver_commander.get(did)
698:                 reloc_target = self._driver_relocation_target.get(did)
699: 
700:                 if reloc_target is not None and commander is not None and commander != reloc_target:
701:                     # Relocation is a cooperative transfer: sender and receiver share the empty travel cost 50/50
702:                     local_rewards[commander] -= 0.5 * cfg.beta * empty_km
703:                     if 0 <= reloc_target < self._num_zones:
704:                         local_rewards[reloc_target] -= 0.5 * cfg.beta * empty_km
705:                 elif commander is not None and 0 <= commander < self._num_zones:
706:                     local_rewards[commander] -= cfg.beta * empty_km
707:                 else:
708:                     if self._last_obs is not None and did in self._last_obs:
709:                         d = self._last_obs[did]["self"]
710:                         zr = max(0, min(int(d.get("current_region", 0)), self._num_zones - 1))
711:                         local_rewards[zr] -= cfg.beta * empty_km
712:                     else:
713:                         local_rewards -= (cfg.beta * empty_km) / self._num_zones
714: 
715:         # 3. Pending waiting time penalty (-alpha) — volume-scaled (D15)
716:         if self._current_pending:
717:             zone_wait_times: dict[int, list[float]] = {
718:                 zi: [] for zi in range(self._num_zones)
719:             }
720:             for o in self._current_pending:
721:                 orig = np.asarray(o["origin"], dtype=float)
722:                 zi = int(np.argmin(np.sum((cents - orig) ** 2, axis=1)))
723:                 zone_wait_times[zi].append(float(o.get("waiting_time", 0.0)))
724:             for zi in range(self._num_zones):
725:                 if zone_wait_times[zi]:
726:                     w_sum_norm = float(np.sum(zone_wait_times[zi])) / max(cfg.order_timeout, 1e-9)
727:                     # D22: removed the hidden extra x0.1 (now folded into
728:                     # alpha=0.1 default) so the config value is the true weight.
729:                     local_rewards[zi] -= cfg.alpha * w_sum_norm
730: 
731:         # 4. Cancellation penalty (D23: Spatially-shared with neighbor surplus)
732:         cancel_penalty = float(getattr(cfg, "cancel_penalty", 0.5))
733:         if cancel_penalty > 0.0:
734:             current_cancelled = {
735:                 oid for oid, o in self.env.orders.items()
736:                 if o.status == OrderStatus.CANCELLED
737:             }
738:             newly_cancelled = current_cancelled - getattr(self, "_seen_cancelled_ids", set())
739:             if not hasattr(self, "_seen_cancelled_ids"):
740:                 self._seen_cancelled_ids = set()
741:             self._seen_cancelled_ids.update(newly_cancelled)
742:             for oid in newly_cancelled:
743:                 order = self.env.orders.get(oid)
744:                 if order is not None:
745:                     orig = np.asarray(order.origin, dtype=float)
746:                     zi = int(np.argmin(np.sum((cents - orig) ** 2, axis=1)))
747:                     # Find neighbors of zi
748:                     neighbours = list(self.env.region_neighbours[zi]) if (hasattr(self.env, "region_neighbours") and zi < len(self.env.region_neighbours)) else []
749:                     # Check idle counts in neighbors
750:                     idle_neighbours = [n for n in neighbours if n < self._num_zones and self._zone_idle_counts[n] > 0]
751:                     if idle_neighbours:
752:                         # Zone zi pays 50%, neighbors with idle cars split remaining 50%
753:                         local_rewards[zi] -= 0.5 * cancel_penalty
754:                         penalty_per_n = 0.5 * cancel_penalty / len(idle_neighbours)
755:                         for n in idle_neighbours:
756:                             local_rewards[n] -= penalty_per_n
757:                     else:
758:                         local_rewards[zi] -= cancel_penalty
759:                 else:
760:                     local_rewards -= cancel_penalty / self._num_zones
761: 
762:         # 5. Team cooperation blending (e.g. 80% local + 20% global team)
763:         team_share = float(getattr(cfg, "team_share", 0.2))
764:         if team_share > 0.0:
765:             mean_r = float(np.mean(local_rewards))
766:             blended_rewards = (1.0 - team_share) * local_rewards + team_share * mean_r
767:         else:
768:             blended_rewards = local_rewards
769: 
770:         return blended_rewards
771: 
772:     def _compute_reward(self, events: dict[int, dict]) -> float:
773:         """Scalar global mean reward (kept for backward compatibility)."""
774:         return float(np.mean(self._compute_zone_rewards(events)))
775: 
776:     def _flush_turn(self) -> None:
777:         if self._terminated or self._last_obs is None:
778:             return

452: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
453: # Parallel rollout workers.
454: #
455: # Parallelism is across EPISODES, never across zones within a turn: zones in
456: # one turn share a single RideGym instance (claimed-order set, FIFO driver
457: # queues, one _flush_turn, zone reward attribution) and must stay sequential.
458: # Each worker owns an independent RideGymAEC copy, runs the standard
459: # sequential AEC loop (with the same per-turn zone batching as
460: # collect_rollout), and returns its buffer shard. The parent concatenates
461: # shards and runs the PPO update. Learning semantics are unchanged — same
462: # MDP, just more diverse samples per update.
463: # Workers run on CPU (spawn context; no CUDA in forks) and receive the
464: # current policy weights each iteration.
465: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
466: 
467: _WORKER_CTX: dict = {}
468: 
469: 
470: def _worker_init(
471:     env_name: str,
472:     env_cfg: EnvConfig,
473:     algo_name: str,
474:     algo_cfg: IPPOConfig | MAPPOConfig,
475:     observation_dim: int,
476:     action_dim: int,
477:     state_dim: int | None,
478:     use_state: bool,
479: ) -> None:
480:     """Build one persistent env + CPU learner per worker process."""
481:     # One thread per worker: with N workers on N cores, torch's default
482:     # multi-threading oversubscribes and slows everything down.
483:     torch.set_num_threads(1)
484:     env, _, _ = _make_env(env_name, 0, None, base_env_cfg=env_cfg)
485:     cpu = torch.device("cpu")
486:     if use_state:
487:         assert isinstance(algo_cfg, MAPPOConfig)
488:         learner: IPPO | MAPPO = MAPPO(
489:             observation_dim=observation_dim,
490:             state_dim=state_dim or 0,
491:             action_dim=action_dim,
492:             config=algo_cfg,
493:             device=cpu,
494:         )
495:     else:
496:         assert isinstance(algo_cfg, IPPOConfig)

902:     def _kpi_counts(self) -> dict[str, float]:
903:         from ride_gym.enums import OrderStatus
904: 
905:         served = sum(
906:             1 for o in self.env.orders.values()
907:             if o.status == OrderStatus.COMPLETED
908:         )
909:         cancelled = sum(
910:             1 for o in self.env.orders.values()
911:             if o.status == OrderStatus.CANCELLED
912:         )
913:         wait_times = [
914:             float(o.pickup_time - o.request_time)
915:             for o in self.env.orders.values()
916:             if o.status == OrderStatus.COMPLETED and o.pickup_time is not None
917:         ]
918:         avg_wait = float(np.mean(wait_times)) if wait_times else 0.0
919:         empty_km = float(self._episode_empty_m / _M_PER_KM)
920:         return {
921:             "served": float(served),
922:             "cancelled": float(cancelled),
923:             "avg_wait": avg_wait,
924:             "empty_km": empty_km,
925:         }
926: 
927:     def kpi_summary(self) -> dict[str, float]:
928:         """Return episode summary KPIs (served, cancelled, avg_wait, empty_km)."""
929:         return self._kpi_counts()
930: 
931:     # -------------------------------------------------------------------- AEC
932: 
933:     def step(self, action: int | None) -> None:
934:         if not self.agents:
935:             return
936:         if self._terminated:
937:             done_agent = self.agent_selection
938:             remaining = [a for a in self.agents if a != done_agent]
939:             self.rewards.pop(done_agent, None)
940:             self._cumulative_rewards[done_agent] = 0.0
941:             self.agents = remaining
942:             self.agent_selection = remaining[0] if remaining else None
943:             self.terminations = {a: True for a in remaining}
944:             self.truncations = {a: False for a in remaining}
945:             self.infos = {a: {} for a in remaining}
946:             return
947:         current = self.agent_selection
948:         self.rewards[current] = 0.0
949:         self._cumulative_rewards[current] = 0.0
950:         act_val = 0 if action is None else int(action)
951:         self._actions_this_turn[current] = act_val
952:         if 1 <= act_val <= self._max_pending:
953:             zone_idx = self._zone_int_id(current)
954:             candidates = self._zone_candidate_orders[zone_idx] if zone_idx < len(self._zone_candidate_orders) else []
955:             cand_idx = act_val - 1
956:             if cand_idx < len(candidates):
957:                 if not hasattr(self, "_claimed_orders_this_turn"):
958:                     self._claimed_orders_this_turn = set()
959:                 self._claimed_orders_this_turn.add(candidates[cand_idx]["order_id"])
960:         self._advance_selection()
961: 
962:     def state(self) -> np.ndarray:
963:         """Centralized state (for MAPPO's shared critic): all-zone fleet state
964:         + pending-order features + clock."""
965:         if self._last_obs is None:
966:             return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float32)
967:         return np.concatenate(
968:             [
969:                 self._zone_fleet_state.flatten(),
970:                 self._pending_features.flatten(),
971:                 self._pending_mask,
972:                 np.array(
973:                     [min(self.env.time / max(self.env.horizon, 1e-9), 1.0)],
974:                     dtype=np.float32,
975:                 ),
976:             ]
977:         ).astype(np.float32)
978: 
979:     def close(self) -> None:
980:         if hasattr(self.env, "close"):
981:             self.env.close()

## Assistant

_Thinking:_



Proper infra first — no metrics, no training. What is structurally wrong:

**A. Environment — action semantics unstable**

- `pettingzoo_env.py:134` — action space `1+max_pending+num_zones` (93 NYC). Dispatch `1..max_pending` indexes into per-zone candidate list rebuilt every turn (`:441-456`, lexsort distance/wait, strict zone ownership + 2km radius). Same index = different `order_id` next turn. Policy learns position, not order. Stationarity broken.
- `pettingzoo_env.py:600-611` — `_decode_action` fallback to global `_current_pending[idx]` if zone list short. Violates D23 strict ownership. Masked today, fragile.
- Relocate decode `:612-615` accepts any zone, no neighbor check. Mask (`:586-595`) enforces neighbor + surplus, decode does not. Illegal hop executes if mask drifts.

**B. Environment — most turns are forced no-op**

- `action_mask:568-596` — dispatch needs dispatchable>0, relocate needs idle>0 + target not surplus. 100 drivers / 62 zones ≈1.6/zone. Majority of zone-turns have 0 idle → only action 0 legal. Huge idle imbalance → PPO collapses to idle, relocation never sampled enough to learn.

**C. Environment — turn order + batching mismatch**

- `_flush_turn:801-804` walks `self._agent_order` (reshuffled each turn `:896`), FIFO driver queues, first-come-first-served. Earlier in shuffle gets first driver/order. Extra within-turn non-stationarity.
- `train.py:360-362,393-420` — Phase 1 batches obs/mask for all zones pre-turn, Phase 2 steps sequentially. Later agents' masks miss earlier agents' `_claimed_orders_this_turn` from same turn. Eval (`shift_eval`, `evaluate`) steps sequentially with fresh mask. Train/eval mismatch.

**D. Environment — obs missing what relocation needs**

- `pettingzoo_env.py:336-350`, `encoder.py:24-34` — flat MLP input, no adjacency. `region_neighbours` used in mask but never observed. Agent must infer legal moves from trial.
- No recent demand / forecast / ETA. Brief requires current + recent demand. `time_frac` only clock. Timeout 2.0min = 4 turns, neighbor hop 2-4min = 4-8 turns (`config.py:63-65`, D26). Reactive window shorter than proactive move time — relocation cannot rescue current pending, only predict future, but no predictive signal given.

**E. Loop — credit structure punishes relocation by construction**

- Dispatch credited at assignment (`:661-672`), not completion. Assignment that later cancels still paid.
- Empty cost shared 50/50 sender/receiver (`:700-704`). Receiver penalized for being helped — pollutes its value.
- Cancel shared with idle neighbours (`:750-756`). Holding idle reserve (needed for relocation) gets punished for another zone's backlog.
- D28 arrival bonus (`:674-689`, `:849-859`) pays twice with 30% dispatch assist, uses `pending_per_zone` already /num_drivers — scale unverified. Double-pay needs justification or removal.
- `team_share` blending (`:762-768`) adds global mean noise to 62 local signals.

**F. Loop — correctness gaps, no tests**

- No `api_test`, no mask↔decode consistency test, no reward-attribution test, no determinism test. `tests/` does not cover AEC semantics.
- `collect_rollout:278-326` drain steps extra RideGym ticks after KPIs accounted; rewards from drain flush dropped, time advances. Untested.
- `total_turns` must equal k×episode_turns or GAE bootstraps with own value (`ippo.py` boundary). Not enforced.
- No obs/value normalization. Fractions 0-0.02 vs coords 0-1 into Tanh MLP 256×2 — saturation risk.
- Spaces not frozen: `max_pending` 80→30 changes obs/action dims, old checkpoints mismatch. Eval carries weight-shape hacks — violates spaces-freeze rule.

**G. Scale / protocol blockers for a proper study**

- `config.py:163` 100 drivers vs `demand-shift.md:5` 50 drivers. Run-of-record ambiguous.
- `nyc_window_limit` 500 vs 15k/hr real rate. Uniform vs block sampling contradiction (D23 vs `UniformSampled...` name). Must lock sampling + window budget before any arch comparison.
- Single training seed, 93-action head, PPO defaults copied (epochs 4, minibatch 4, lr 3e-4, entropy 0.01). No LR schedule, no entropy schedule agreed (D19 vs D28 conflict).
- Serial rollout default (`num_workers=1`). 5 seeds × 2 algos × 200it × 6ep × 120 turns × 62 agents ≈ 44k transitions/iter — days serial. Parallel + determinism harness required before architecture search.
- Protocol needs: identical train/val/test instances across methods, 5 training seeds, frozen deterministic eval, time-over-episode curves, random floor. None locked yet.

Proposed order: freeze spaces + fix decode/mask parity → add adjacency + recent-demand to obs → fix credit (single arrival pay, remove receiver penalty) → align timescales (timeout vs hop) → unit tests (api_test, determinism, attribution) → then architecture choice, then training.

{
  "content": "# Action audit — Phase 1 (inspection only)\n\n## 1. Positional dispatch: fine as a ranking rule\nCandidates are re-sorted every turn (`_rebuild_caches`, lexsort distance/wait),\nbut the obs lists the same order (`pending_orders` = same sorted features).\nSo action k always means \"k-th ranked candidate\" with matching obs row.\nStationary. No fix.\n\n## 2. Mask/decode mismatch: fix decode\nMask enforces neighbor-only + surplus-target + claimed-order checks\n(`action_mask`). Decode (`_decode_action`) enforces none of them and falls\nback to the global pending list, breaking zone ownership. PPO masks with\n-inf so on-policy behavior is masked-correct, but any mask bug executes an\nillegal hop instead of noop. Fix: decode returns None for non-neighbor\ntargets, drop the global fallback. Needs decisions.md entry + bump\n(behavior change for off-mask actions only).\n\n## 3. Forced noop: accept, mitigate later\n~1.6 drivers/zone means most zone-turns have 0 idle → only action 0 legal.\nInherent to one-action-per-zone-turn. Not fixable without multi-driver\nactions (advisor call, D13 note). Mitigation is reward/entropy, Phase 3.\n\n## 4. Train/eval mask staleness: real, loop fix\nTrain batches all masks pre-turn, so later agents miss same-turn claims;\neval steps sequentially with fresh masks. Unify in Phase 4 (fresh mask\nper step in `collect_rollout`).\n",

}
